Conclusion: ForMaT为开发布局感知的翻译模型提供了基准测试平台，这类模型需要整合视觉和文本上下文以实现高保真的文档重建。

Abstract: We present ForMaT (Format-Preserving Multilingual Translation), a parallel corpus of 3,956 PDFs across 15 language pairs that preserves original layout metadata proposed for multimodal machine translation. To ensure structural diversity in the dataset, we employ K-Medoids sampling over 45 geometric features, capturing complex elements like nested tables and formulas to focus only on visually diverse PDF documents. Our evaluation reveals that current MT systems struggle with spatial grounding and geometric synchronization, often losing the link between text and its visual context. ForMaT provides a benchmark for developing layout-aware translation models that integrate visual and textual context for high-fidelity document reconstruction.

</details>


### [113] [Towards Generalization of Block Attention via Automatic Segmentation and Block Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15913)
*Shuaiyi Li,Zhisong Zhang,Yan Wang,Lei Zhu,Dongyang Ma,Chenlong Deng,Yang Deng,Wai Lam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出语义分割数据集SemanticSeg和块蒸馏框架，解决了块注意力机制在文本分割和微调效率方面的两大挑战，实现了接近全注意力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块注意力机制在长上下文场景（如RAG）中具有提升KV缓存重用的潜力，但面临两个关键挑战：(1) 难以将输入文本分割成有意义的、自包含的块；(2) 现有的块微调方法效率低下且可能损害性能。

Method: (1) 构建SemanticSeg数据集（30k+实例，16类别，长度2k-32k），训练轻量级分割器实现类人直觉的块分割；(2) 提出块蒸馏训练框架，包含三个创新组件：块汇合令牌（缓解边界信息损失）、块丢弃（利用所有块的训练信号）、令牌级损失加权（聚焦块注意力敏感令牌）。

Result: 在多种模型和基准上，分割器优于启发式和统计基线；块蒸馏在块注意力下实现了接近全注意力的性能。

Conclusion: 建立了实用且可扩展的块注意力部署路径，为长上下文场景下的高效推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Block attention, which processes the input as separate blocks that cannot attend to one another, offers significant potential to improve KV cache reuse in long-context scenarios such as Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). However, its broader application is hindered by two key challenges: the difficulty of segmenting input text into meaningful, self-contained blocks, and the inefficiency of existing block fine-tuning methods that risk degrading performance. To address these, we first construct SemanticSeg, a large and diverse semantic segmentation dataset containing over 30k instances across 16 categories-including books, code, web text, and conversations with text lengths ranging from 2k to 32k. Using this dataset, we train a lightweight segmenter to automatically partition text into human-instinct-aligned blocks with controllable granularity. Second, we propose block distillation, a training framework that is more efficient than block fine-tuning, which uses a frozen full-attention teacher model to guide the block-attention student. This framework integrates three novel components: block sink tokens to mitigate information loss at block boundaries, block dropout to leverage training signals from all blocks, and token-level loss weighting to focus learning on block-attention-sensitive tokens. Experiments across multiple models and benchmarks demonstrate that our segmenter outperforms heuristic and statistical baselines, and block distillation achieves near-full-attention performance under block attention, establishing a practical and scalable pathway for deploying block attention.

</details>


### [114] [Reference-Free Reinforcement Learning Fine-Tuning for MT: A Seq2Seq Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15976)
*Ernesto Garcia-Estrada,Carlos Escolano,José A. R. Fonallosa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统地研究了将组相对策略优化（GRPO）应用于编码器-解码器架构的机器翻译模型（NLLB-200，600M和1.3B参数），使用混合无参奖励（LaBSE和COMET-Kiwi），在13种语言上取得一致改进（最高+5.03 chrF++），且无需任何平行数据微调，在形态复杂语言上与3轮有监督微调竞争，并发现增益与基线性能负相关、与奖励可区分性正相关的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习MT微调主要针对解码器-only大语言模型（≥7B），缺乏对编码器-解码器架构的系统性研究。

Method: 将GRPO应用于NLLB-200（600M和1.3B），使用LaBSE和COMET-Kiwi的混合无参考奖励，无需平行数据微调，在13种语言上评估。

Result: GRPO在13种语言上均取得一致改进，最高+5.03 chrF++（繁体中文），在形态复杂语言上与3轮有监督微调竞争；增益在基线最弱且奖励可区分性最高时最大。

Conclusion: GRPO对编码器-解码器架构有效，尤其在平行数据稀缺的低资源场景下最具优势，该模式在英语和西班牙语源语言上均可复现。

Abstract: Production machine translation relies overwhelmingly on encoder-decoder Seq2Seq models, yet reinforcement learning approaches to MT fine-tuning have largely targeted decoder-only LLMs at $\geq$7B parameters, with limited systematic study of encoder-decoder architectures. We apply Group Relative Policy Optimization to NLLB-200 (600M and 1.3B) using a hybrid reference-free reward (LaBSE and COMET-Kiwi) that requires no parallel data at fine-tuning time, evaluating across 13 typologically diverse languages. GRPO yields consistent improvements on all 13 languages, up to $+$5.03 chrF++ for Traditional Chinese, and, without any target-language data, competes with 3-epoch supervised fine-tuning on morphologically complex languages . We identify a consistent empirical pattern in which gains are largest where baseline performance is weakest and reward discriminability is highest, making this approach most effective precisely where parallel data is scarcest, and replicate this pattern across English and Spanish source languages.

</details>


### [115] [Ontology for Policing: Conceptual Knowledge Learning for Semantic Understanding and Reasoning in Law Enforcement Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15978)
*Anita Srbinovska,Jansen Orfan,Adrian Martin,Ernest Fokoué*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个符号化框架，用于将执法报告中的叙述文本转化为基于证据的事实，并通过时间线索和领域公理构建时间图。在450份财产犯罪报告上评估，54.1%的提取事件置信度≥0.80，93.7%通过语义路径映射成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执法报告中的关键事实以自然语言叙述形式存在，需要人工阅读才能提取，缺乏自动化方法将非结构化文本转化为可供审查、培训与调查使用的结构化证据。

Method: 采用符号化方法：个人标识符脱敏、语义解析、谓词映射到本体（PropBank→VerbNet→WordNet路径）、推理，并利用时间线索和领域公理构建时间图。

Result: 系统提取事件中54.1%置信度≥0.80；93.7%事件通过PropBank-VerbNet-WordNet语义路径成功映射；事件发起、被盗物品和时间线索的注释一致性达100%，强行进入解释的一致性较低。

Conclusion: 符号化方法能有效从执法报告叙述中提取结构化事实，在关键事件上达到高准确率和一致性，但在语义模糊的判断（如强行进入）上仍有局限。

Abstract: Law enforcement reports contain structured fields and written narratives. However, many incident facts that are needed for review, police training, and investigations are in natural language and require manual reading. We propose a framework using symbolic methods for converting narratives into evidence-linked facts. Our objective is to measure the value of narratives to recover incident details only from the unstructured text and build temporal graphs with time cues and domain axioms. We achieve this by redacting personal identifiers, semantic parsing, predicate mapping to ontology, and reasoning. We evaluate the symbolic approach on 450 property crime reports and a short human review. Of the extracted events from the system, 54.1% had a confidence score of at least 0.80 and 93.7% were mapped through the PropBank--VerbNet--WordNet semantic path. 100% agreement was reached on incident initiation, stolen items, and temporal cues and lower agreement for forced entry interpretation.

</details>


### [116] [Defining Cultural Capabilities for AI Evaluation: A Taxonomy Grounded in Intercultural Communication Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15990)
*Isar Nejadgholi,Masoud Kianpour,Krishnapriya Vishnubhotla,Maryam Molamohamadi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出当前AI系统文化能力评估存在概念模糊、术语混用、仅局限于回忆事实性知识等问题。为此，借鉴跨文化传播学理论，提出了AI相关文化能力的三层次分类体系：文化意识（模型是否知道）、文化敏感性（模型如何呈现知识）、文化能力（模型能否在交互中自适应调整），旨在为多文化环境下的AI评估提供更有效的概念框架和实践工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对AI系统文化能力的评估中，文化能力的概念定义模糊、术语混用，且评估内容通常仅局限于回忆关于不同人口、地区和国籍的准确事实性信息，缺乏清晰的构念界定，导致评估结果可能高估模型能力，进而在文化敏感场景中导致不恰当的部署决策。

Method: 借鉴跨文化传播学（Intercultural Communication）的理论成果，提出一个三层次的文化能力分类体系：（1）文化意识——模型是否知道相关文化知识；（2）文化敏感性——模型如何框架化/呈现其所知的知识；（3）文化能力——模型能否随着交互的演进进行自适应调整。

Result: 提出了一个三层次的文化能力分类体系（文化意识、文化敏感性、文化能力），明确了各层次的区分，并指出该分类体系可作为提升AI评估在实际多文化场景中有效性和可解释性的实用工具。

Conclusion: 只有明确文化能力的构念定义，才能避免评估结果误导模型能力判断，防止在文化敏感场景中出现不恰当的部署决策。该分类体系为AI系统在真实多文化环境中的评估提供了更清晰的概念基础和实用性工具。

Abstract: Tremendous efforts have been put into evaluating the inclusivity and effectiveness of AI systems across cultures. However, the cultural capabilities considered in much of the literature remain vaguely defined, are referred to using interchangeable terminology, and are typically limited to recalling accurate information about various demographics, regions, and nationalities. To address this construct ambiguity, we draw from Intercultural Communication scholarship and propose a three-level taxonomy of AI-relevant cultural capabilities: Cultural Awareness answers "Does the model know?", Cultural Sensitivity answers "How does it frame its knowledge?", and Cultural Competence answers "Can it adapt as the interaction evolves?". Beyond conceptual clarification, we position this taxonomy as a practical tool for improving the validity and interpretability of AI evaluation in real-world, multicultural settings. Without such construct clarity, evaluation results risk overstating model capabilities and may lead to inappropriate deployment decisions in culturally sensitive contexts.

</details>


### [117] [Can Vision Language Models Be Adaptive in Mathematics Education? A Learner Model-based Rubric Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16011)
*Jie Gao,Yongan Yu,Junzhu Su,Yiran Lin,Adam K. Dube,Jackie Chi Kit Cheung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉语言模型（VLMs）在数学教学中的适应性评估问题，基于自适应学习框架中的学习者模型，提出了一个包含认知、动机和复杂度三个方面的评估标准，并额外评估回答的正确性和质量。实验表明，当前VLMs在生成基于学习者模型的个性化教学响应方面存在困难，尤其是在学习者信息有限的情况下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型缺乏针对数学教学任务中适应不同学习者画像能力的系统评估框架，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 基于自适应学习框架中的学习者模型，提出了一套包含认知、动机和复杂度三个方面的评估标准，并额外评估回答的正确性和质量。

Result: 不同模型在适应性上存在可测量的差异；当前VLMs难以持续生成基于学习者模型的教学响应，尤其在获取有限学习者信息时表现不佳。

Conclusion: 当前VLMs在数学辅导任务中对不同学习者画像的适应能力不足，现有评估框架可为此类能力的系统评测提供支撑。

Abstract: Adaptive learning refers to educational technologies that track learners' learning progress and adapt the instructional process based on individual learners' learning performance. It is increasingly recognized as critical for developing an effective learning support tool. Vision language models (VLMs) have seen adoption in mathematics education, and students have been using them as learning aids for personalized instruction. However, it is unknown whether VLMs have the ability to adapt to different learner profiles when providing mathematical instructions. Current VLMs lack a systematic evaluation framework for this adaptivity to different learner profiles in mathematics tutoring tasks. To address this gap, we draw on the learner model from the adaptive learning framework (Shute and Towle, 2018) and propose a learner model-based rubric. Our rubric formalizes adaptivity assessment into three aspects: cognitive aspects, motivational aspects, and complexity. We also evaluate two additional dimensions of VLM responses: correctness (of answers and solutions) and quality (of the response itself). Our experimental results show measurable differences in adaptivity across models and also reveal that current VLMs struggle to consistently produce learner model-based instructional responses, especially when receiving limited learner information.

</details>


### [118] [Judge Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16023)
*Nils Feldhus,Tanja Baeumel,Elena Golimblevskaia,Qianli Wang,Van Bach Nguyen,Aaron Louis Eidt,Christopher Ebert,Wojciech Samek,Jing Yang,Vera Schmitt,Sebastian Möller,Simon Ostermann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM评判在不同格式下存在系统性偏差，其根源在于共享的潜在评估器子图与脆弱的格式特定终端分支之间的结构解耦，导致格式无关的偏好信号在下游被格式隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge范式下，同一模型在不同输出格式（如1-5评分 vs 真/假标签）下给出系统性不同的评分，现有诊断仅停留在输入-输出层面，缺乏对内部机制的因果解释。

Method: 使用位置感知边缘归因修补（PEAP）对Gemma-3、Qwen2.5和Llama-3进行因果机制分析，定位并零消融潜在评估器子图，结构解耦抽象评估与输出格式化。

Result: 结构理解与开放偏好任务共享一个稀疏的、泛化的潜在评估器子图（位于中后期MLP层）；零消融该子图会破坏评判能力但保留世界知识；格式不一致源于共享主干中连续的评判信号通过脆弱的格式特定终端分支映射。

Conclusion: 基准级别的跨格式可靠性比较部分衡量的是格式化器几何结构而非评估质量；格式无关的偏好可以在下游被独立提取。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge has become the dominant paradigm for grading model outputs at scale, yet the same model assigns systematically different scores when its output format changes (e.g., a 1-5 rating vs. a True/False label). Existing diagnoses of these format-induced inconsistencies stop at the input-output level. Using Position-aware Edge Attribution Patching (PEAP), we causally investigate the internal mechanism in Gemma-3, Qwen2.5, and Llama-3. We find that judgments across structured understanding and open-ended preference tasks share a sparse, generalized Latent Evaluator sub-graph in the mid-to-late multi-layer perceptrons (MLPs); zero-ablating it collapses judgment while preserving world knowledge in architecturally modular models. By structurally decoupling abstract judging from output formatting, we provide a mechanistic account of format-induced inconsistency on the open-weight models we study: a continuous judgment signal computed in the shared trunk is mapped through fragile, format-specific terminal branches, enabling format-independent preference to be isolated downstream of the requested output format. Our findings imply that benchmark-level reliability comparisons across formats are partially measuring formatter geometry rather than evaluation quality.

</details>


### [119] [From Flat Language Labels to Typological Priors: Structured Language Conditioning for Multilingual Speech-to-Speech Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16026)
*Yu Pan,Yang Hou,Xiongfei Wu,Liang Zhang,Yves Le Traon,Lei Ma,Jianjun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出S2ST-Omni 2框架，利用类型学先验知识替代平面语言标签，在多个层次（表示层、声学层、解码层）改进多语言语音到语音翻译，在低资源场景下表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有S2ST系统或忽略源语言信息，或采用"语言即标签"范式将每种语言编码为独立平面嵌入，忽视了跨语言共享的系统性语言结构，导致在有监督数据稀缺时多语言自适应效率受限。

Method: 提出S2ST-Omni 2框架，在三个层面重构语言条件化：(1)类型学驱动的层次化语言编码实现结构化源语言表示；(2)动态门控的语言感知Dual-CTC实现内容自适应声学调制；(3)类型学感知的LLM提示实现解码端语言引导。

Result: 在CVSS-C数据集上，S2ST-Omni 2在BLEU、COMET、ASR-BLEU和BLASER 2.0指标上均优于代表性S2ST方法。消融实验验证了三个层面策略的互补性。仅用约3小时日语-英语监督数据即可实现有效迁移。

Conclusion: 显式类型学先验为数据高效的多语言S2ST提供了有用的归纳偏置，尤其在低资源场景下效果显著。

Abstract: Compositional speech-to-speech translation (S2ST) systems built upon speech large language models (SpeechLLMs) have recently shown promising performance. However, existing S2ST systems often either neglect source-language information or encode it through a language-as-label paradigm, representing each source language as an independent flat embedding. Such a design overlooks systematic linguistic structure shared across languages, which may limit data-efficient multilingual adaptation when supervised S2ST data are scarce. To address this issue, we propose S2ST-Omni 2, a many-to-one compositional S2ST framework that systematically reformulates multilingual language conditioning from flat language labels to structured typological priors. Specifically, S2ST-Omni 2 revisits language conditioning at three levels: typology-informed hierarchical language encoding for structured source-language representation, dynamically-gated language-aware Dual-CTC for content-adaptive acoustic modulation, and typology-aware LLM prompting for decoder-side linguistic guidance. Experiments on CVSS-C show that S2ST-Omni 2 achieves superior average performance among representative S2ST approaches across BLEU, COMET, ASR-BLEU, and BLASER 2.0 under the adopted evaluation protocol. Ablation studies indicate that the proposed representation-level, acoustic-level, and decoding-level strategies provide complementary benefits. Moreover, controlled data-budget analyses and a Japanese-to-English evaluation using only approximately 3 hours of supervised training data suggest that explicit typological priors provide useful inductive biases for data-efficient multilingual S2ST.

</details>


### [120] [RecMem: Recurrence-based Memory Consolidation for Efficient and Effective Long-Running LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16045)
*Zijie Dai,Shiyuan Deng,Sheng Guan,Yizhou Tian,Xin Yao,Xiao Yan,James Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RecMem提出了一种基于循环触发的记忆整合机制，通过潜意识存储层和轻量级嵌入模型减少LLM调用，在降低高达87%令牌消耗的同时提升记忆系统准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统对每个交互都调用LLM进行记忆提取（急切的记忆整合），导致大量令牌消耗，亟需一种更高效的记忆整合策略。

Method: 1) 将交互存储于潜意识记忆层，使用轻量级嵌入模型编码；2) 仅当检测到语义相似交互的持续循环时，才调用LLM提取情景记忆和语义记忆；3) 引入语义精炼机制恢复被记忆提取遗漏的细粒度事实。

Result: 在三个SOTA记忆系统上，RecMem将记忆构建的令牌成本降低高达87%，同时准确性超过原系统。

Conclusion: 基于循环触发的记忆整合策略能有效平衡记忆构建效率与准确性，通过有选择地调用LLM处理高信息量的语义簇，大幅降低计算开销。

Abstract: Memory systems often organize user-agent interactions as retrievable external memory and are crucial for long-running agents by overcoming the limited context windows of LLMs. However, existing memory systems invoke LLMs to process every incoming interaction for memory extraction, and such an eager memory consolidation scheme leads to substantial token consumption. To tackle this problem, we propose RecMem by rethinking when memory consolidation should be conducted. RecMem stores incoming interactions in a subconscious memory layer and encode them using lightweight embedding models for retrieval. LLMs are only invoked to extract episodic and semantic memory when sustained recurrence are observed for semantically similar interactions. Such recurrence-based consolidation works because these interactions correspond to a semantic cluster with rich information and thus are worth extraction and summarization. To improve accuracy, RecMem also incorporates a semantic refinement mechanism that recovers the fine-grained facts omitted by memory extraction. Experiments show that RecMem reduces the memory construction token cost of three SOTA memory systems by up to 87% while exceeding their accuracy.

</details>


### [121] [Can Large Language Models Imitate Human Speech for Clinical Assessment? LLM-Driven Data Augmentation for Cognitive Score Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16077)
*Si-Belkacem Yamine Ketir,Lenard Paulo Tamayo,Shohei Hisada,Shaowen Peng,Shoko Wakamiya,Eiji Aramaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用GPT-5生成多样化口语式独白的LLM驱动数据增强框架，结合相似度引导的类别平衡选择策略，在日语语料库上通过Sentence-BERT嵌入和偏最小二乘回归提升Hasegawa痴呆量表评分预测性能，有效缓解小样本和类别不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自发性语音中准确评估认知衰退仍面临数据集规模有限和类别不平衡的挑战，现有方法难以在少数类（低认知得分）样本上取得稳定可靠的预测效果。

Method: 1) 使用GPT-5以书面回答为语义锚点，生成多种风格的口语式独白进行数据增强；2) 提取Sentence-BERT语音嵌入；3) 采用偏最小二乘回归预测Hasegawa痴呆量表得分；4) 对比两种增强策略：随机类别平衡选择和相似度引导的类别平衡选择。

Result: 随机类别平衡选择带来中等但不稳定的改进；相似度引导的类别平衡选择实现了更一致的性能提升，显著降低了少数低分参与者的预测误差，同时保持了多数群体的预测性能。

Conclusion: 语义引导的LLM驱动数据增强是解决临床语音分析中类别不平衡和数据效率问题的有效原则性方法，具有推广潜力。

Abstract: Accurate assessment of cognitive decline from spontaneous speech remains challenging due to limited dataset size and class imbalance. In this work, we propose a large language model (LLM)-driven data augmentation framework to improve the prediction of cognitive scores from speech. Experiments are conducted on a Japanese corpus in which each participant provides both a spontaneous oral narrative and a written response to the same clinical prompt. The written responses serve as semantic anchors to generate multiple oral-like monologues in different styles using GPT-5. We then predict Hasegawa Dementia Scale scores, a widely used cognitive screening tool in Japan, using a Partial Least Squares regression model trained on Sentence-BERT speech embeddings. We investigate two augmentation strategies: random class-balanced selection, which yields moderate but unstable improvements, and similarity-guided class-balanced selection. The latter prioritizes semantically close synthetic samples, leading to more consistent improvements and substantially reducing prediction error for minority low-score participants while maintaining performance for the majority group. Overall, our findings demonstrate the potential of semantically guided LLM-driven augmentation as a principled approach for addressing class imbalance and improving data efficiency in clinical speech analysis.

</details>


### [122] [Multi-Level Contextual Token Relation Modeling for Machine-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16107)
*Chenwang Wu,Yiuming Cheung,Bo Han,Shuhai Zhang,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多层级上下c文本Token关系建模框架，通过马尔可夫校准模块（局部关系）和规则支撑推理模块（全局关系）来改进基于度量的机器生成文本检测方法，有效克服了Token级检测分数易受生成过程随机性偏差的问题，在跨模型和跨领域场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器生成文本带来了虚假信息、钓鱼攻击等风险，现有基于度量的方法虽然比基于模型的方法更实用，但存在一个核心挑战：Token级检测分数容易被MGT生成过程的固有随机性所偏差，影响了检测可靠性。

Method: 1. 将代表性基于度量的方法统一到一个框架中进行评估；2. 理论推导Token级检测分数的多跳转移关系，探索其局部和全局关系；3. 提出多层级上下文Token关系建模框架：局部关系通过轻量级马尔可夫校准模块细化Token级证据，全局关系通过规则支撑推理模块利用上下文分数统计中的显式逻辑规则；4. 联合多层级推理框架融合局部校准分数和全局推理信号。

Result: 在多种真实场景（跨大语言模型、跨领域设置）下取得了广泛且显著的改进，且计算开销较低。

Conclusion: 提出的多层级上下文Token关系建模框架有效解决了基于度量的MGT检测方法中Token级分数偏差问题，兼顾了检测性能与计算效率。

Abstract: Machine-generated texts (MGTs) pose risks such as disinformation and phishing, underscoring the need for reliable detection. Metric-based methods, which extract statistically distinguishable features of MGTs, are often more practical than complex model-based methods that are prone to overfitting. Given their diverse designs, we first place representative metric-based methods within a unified framework, enabling a clear assessment of their advantages and limitations. Our analysis identifies a core challenge across these methods: the token-level detection score is easily biased by the inherent randomness of the MGTs generation process. Then, we theoretically derive the multi-hop transitions of the token-level detection score and explore their local and global relations. Based on these findings, we propose a multi-level contextual token relation modeling framework for MGT detection. Specifically, for local relations, we model them through a lightweight Markov-informed calibration module that refines token-level evidence before aggregation. For global relations, we introduce a rule-support reasoning module that uses explicit logical rules derived from contextual score statistics. Finally, we combine the local calibrated score and the global rule-support reasoning signal in a joint multi-level inference framework. Extensive experiments show broad and substantial improvements across various real-world scenarios, including cross-LLM and cross-domain settings, with low computational overhead.

</details>


### [123] [DebiasRAG: A Tuning-Free Path to Fair Generation in Large Language Models through Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16113)
*Rui Chu,Bingyin Zhao,Thanh Quoc Hung Le,Duy Cao Hoang,Huawei Lin,Ping Li,Weijie Zhao,Khoa D Doan,Yingjie Lao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DebiasRAG，一种基于检索增强生成的无微调、动态查询特定的去偏框架，通过自诊断偏差上下文和梯度更新重排序，在保持LLM原有能力的同时减少社会偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去偏方法（微调、提示工程）需要额外训练资源或领域知识，可能降低LLM原有能力，且缺乏动态去偏上下文支持。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 查询特定去偏候选生成：利用自诊断偏差上下文反向生成去偏上下文；(2) 上下文候选池构建：从RAG文档库检索查询相关上下文；(3) 梯度更新去偏引导上下文片段重排序。

Result: 在保持LLM表征能力等内在属性的同时提升了公平性。

Conclusion: DebiasRAG实现了无需微调、动态查询特定的去偏，有效缓解了LLM的社会偏见问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved unprecedented success due to their exceptional generative capabilities. However, because they depend on knowledge encapsulated from training corpora, they may produce hallucinations, stereotypes, and socially biased content. In particular, LLMs are prone to prejudiced responses involving race, gender, and age, which are collectively referred to as social biases. Prior studies have used fine-tuning and prompt engineering to mitigate such biases in LLMs, but these methods require additional training resources or domain knowledge to design the framework. Moreover, they may degrade the original capabilities of LLMs and often overlook the need for dynamic debiasing contexts for fairer inference. In this paper, we propose DebiasRAG, a novel tuning-free and dynamic query-specific debiasing framework based on retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). DebiasRAG improves fairness while preserving the intrinsic properties of LLMs, such as representation ability. DebiasRAG consists of three stages: (1) query-specific debiasing candidate generation; (2) context candidate pool construction; and (3) gradient-updated debiasing-guided context piece reranking. First, DebiasRAG leverages self-diagnosed bias contexts relevant to the query through regular retrieval, where the bias contexts are prepared offline by the DebiasRAG provider. Given the query-specific bias contexts, DebiasRAG reversely produces debiasing contexts, which are provided as additional fairness constraints for LLM outputs. Second, a regular RAG retrieval process produces query-related contexts from the regular RAG document database, such as a chunked Wikipedia dataset.

</details>


### [124] [SGR: A Stepwise Reasoning Framework for LLMs with External Subgraph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16117)
*Xin Zhang,Yang Cao,Baoxing Wu,Kai Song,Siying Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SGR框架，通过从外部知识库构建查询特定的子图来增强LLM的多步推理能力，在多个基准数据集上取得了优于竞争基线的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在复杂推理和逻辑推断场景中表现有限，其生成过程可能引入无关、噪声或事实不一致的内容，需要一种方法来增强推理的准确性和事实可靠性。

Method: SGR是一个逐步推理框架，首先根据输入问题从外部知识库构建查询特定的子图，然后利用子图的语义结构引导模型逐步推理，最后结合多条推理轨迹得到最终预测。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，SGR相较于竞争基线取得了持续一致的改进，验证了其在提升推理准确性和事实可靠性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将中间推理步骤锚定在结构化外部知识上，SGR能够帮助模型聚焦于相关实体、关系和支持证据，从而有效提升LLM的复杂推理能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities across diverse NLP applications, such as translation, text generation, and question answering. Nevertheless, they remain limited in complex settings that demand deep reasoning and logical inference. Since these models are trained on large-scale text corpora, their generation process may still introduce irrelevant, noisy, or factually inconsistent content. To mitigate this problem, we introduce SGR, a stepwise framework that enhances LLM reasoning through external subgraph generation. SGR builds query-specific subgraphs from external knowledge bases and uses their semantic structure to support multi-step inference. By grounding intermediate reasoning steps in structured external knowledge, the framework helps the model concentrate on relevant entities, relations, and supporting evidence. In particular, SGR first constructs a subgraph tailored to the input question. It then guides the model to reason progressively over the generated structure and combines multiple reasoning trajectories to obtain the final prediction. Experimental results across several benchmark datasets show that SGR achieves consistent improvements over competitive baselines, highlighting its value for improving both reasoning accuracy and factual reliability.

</details>


### [125] [Argus: Evidence Assembly for Scalable Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16217)
*Zhen Zhang,Liangcai Su,Zhuo Chen,Xiang Lin,Haotian Xu,Simon Shaolei Du,Kaiyu Yang,Bo An,Lidong Bing,Xinyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Argus提出了一种将深度研究视为拼图组装的多智能体系统，通过Searcher和Navigator协作，在保持低推理上下文的同时，通过并行搜索和证据图聚合显著提升了复杂信息检索任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究系统通过并行搜索和聚合来扩展推理时间计算，但并行轨迹常复制而非互补证据，导致收益递减并推高聚合上下文至模型极限。

Method: Argus包含两个智能体：Searcher通过ReAct式交互收集子查询的证据踪迹；Navigator维护共享证据图，验证缺失证据、调度Searcher收集、并在完整图上推理生成带来源追踪的最终答案。Navigator通过强化学习训练验证、调度和综合能力，Searcher保持标准ReAct智能体独立训练。

Result: 基于35B-A3B MoE骨干，Argus在单个Searcher下提升5.5分，8个并行Searcher下提升12.7分（平均8个基准）。64个Searcher时在BrowseComp达到86.2，超越所有测试的专有智能体，而Navigator推理上下文保持在21.5K tokens以内。

Conclusion: 将深度研究重构为拼图组装范式，通过证据图驱动的调度与综合，有效解决了并行搜索中的证据重复问题，实现了可扩展的高效推理。

Abstract: Deep research agents have achieved remarkable progress on complex information seeking tasks. Even long ReAct style rollouts explore only a single trajectory, while recent state of the art systems scale inference time compute via parallel search and aggregation. Yet deep research answers are composed of complementary pieces of evidence, which parallel rollouts often duplicate rather than complete, yielding diminishing returns while pushing the aggregation context toward the model's limit. We propose Argus, an agentic system in which a Searcher and a Navigator cooperate to treat deep research as assembling a jigsaw from complementary evidence pieces, rather than brute forcing the whole answer in parallel. The Searcher collects evidence traces for a given sub-query through ReAct-style interaction. The Navigator maintains a shared evidence graph, verifying which pieces are still missing, dispatching Searchers to gather them, and reasoning over the completed graph to produce a source-traced final answer. We train the Navigator with reinforcement learning to verify, dispatch, and synthesize, while independently training the Searcher to remain a standard ReAct agent. The resulting Navigator supports rollouts with a single Searcher or many in parallel without retraining. With both Searcher and Navigator built on a 35B-A3B MoE backbone, Argus gains 5.5 points with a single Searcher and 12.7 points with 8 parallel Searchers, averaged over eight benchmarks. With 64 Searchers it reaches 86.2 on BrowseComp, surpassing every proprietary agent we benchmark, while the Navigator's reasoning context stays under 21.5K tokens.

</details>


### [126] [Artificial Aphasias in Lesioned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16222)
*Nathan Roll,Jill Kries,Laura Gwilliams,Cory Shain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受失语症启发的技术，通过对语言模型参数进行“损毁”（置零）并测量其对临床失语症症状的影响，来刻画LM的功能组织。分析112,426个样本后发现，LM虽能表现出全部失语症症状，但分布与人类显著不同；注意力组件与FFN组件之间存在广泛的症状差异，且深度影响明显：早期层损毁主要导致句法和语义症状，中后期层则更多产生音韵和流畅性缺陷。LM与人类在症状模式上的质性差异表明失语症综合征高度受学习与处理细节影响，而非领域不变的后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 借鉴失语症文献中脑损伤与语言功能因果关系的思路，提出一种通过“损毁”模型参数来刻画语言模型内部功能组织的方法，从而理解LM中不同组件和层次的语言功能分工。

Method: 对五个1B规模的语言模型进行参数“损毁”（将参数置零），并使用Text Aphasia Battery（TAB）诊断工具评估损毁后模型输出呈现的失语症症状，共分析112,426个模型输出，比较不同组件（注意力Q/K/V/O与FFN up/gate/down）及不同层深损毁的效果。

Result: LM能表现出全部失语症症状但分布与人类显著不同；注意力组件与FFN组件之间症状轮廓差异显著，同一机制内组件间差异较弱；早期层损毁主要引发句法和语义症状，中后期层主要引发音韵和流畅性缺陷；某些LM损毁与特定人类失语症类型定量相似，但质性差异显著。

Conclusion: 失语症综合征并非语言处理中断的领域不变后果，而是受到学习与处理细节的高度影响；所提出的方法为理解LM的功能组织提供了新的神经认知视角。

Abstract: Aphasias, selective language impairments which can arise from brain damage, reveal the functional organization of human language by providing causal links between affected brain regions and specific symptom profiles. Drawing on this literature, we introduce an aphasia-inspired technique to characterize the emergent functional organization of language models (LMs). We ``lesion'' (zero-out) model parameters and measure the effects of this intervention against clinical aphasia symptoms, as diagnosed by the Text Aphasia Battery (TAB). When applied to 112,426 outputs from five 1B-scale LMs, the full range of evaluated symptoms surface, but in distributions largely distinct from those of humans. Our method uncovers broad symptom-profile differences between attention components (query, key, value, output) and feed-forward components (up, gate, down), with weaker evidence for differences among components within the same mechanism. We also find an effect of depth, where lesions in early layers disproportionately cause syntactic and semantic symptoms while late-middle layers yield higher rates of phonological and fluency deficits. Although some LM lesions induce quantitatively more similar profiles to some human aphasia types than others, qualitative differences in symptom patterns between LMs and humans suggest that aphasia syndromes are heavily influenced by the details of learning and processing rather than being a domain-invariant consequence of disrupted language processing.

</details>


### [127] [A Unified Generative-AI Framework for Smart Energy Infrastructure: Intelligent Gas Distribution, Utility Billing, Carbon Analytics, and Quantum-Inspired Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16232)
*Pavan Manjunath,Thomas pruefer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了智能计量、生成式人工智能和量子启发组合优化三者融合如何重塑能源公用事业在物理基础设施管理、客户参与和环境责任方面的运作方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对能源领域在基础设施管理、客户互动和环境问责方面面临的挑战，利用新兴技术实现转型。

Method: 融合智能计量、生成式人工智能和量子启发组合优化三种技术方法。

Result: 加速了能源公用事业在物理基础设施管理、客户参与和环境责任方面的变革进程。

Conclusion: 智能计量、生成式AI和量子启发优化的协同作用正推动能源行业向更高效、更智能、更可持续的方向发展。

Abstract: The accelerating convergence of smart metering, generative artificial intelligence, and quantum-inspired combinatorial optimisation is reshaping how energy utilities manage physical infrastructure, customer engagement, and environmental accountability

</details>


### [128] [A Generative AI Framework for Intelligent Utility Billing CO 2 Analytics and Sustainable Resource Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16250)
*Pavan Manjunath,Thomas Pruefer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，将生成式AI账单起草、变压器负荷预测、碳排放核算和负荷调度四种生产级能力集成在同一架构下，支持可读账单、碳排放追踪和电网压力调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电公司面临三大新要求：向客户提供可读账单、为每度电附加可辩护的碳排放数据、根据电网压力和排放约束调度负荷。现有系统缺乏统一解决方案。

Method: 提出端到端统一框架，包含：(1)生成式AI智能体，基于结构化数值输入在约束解码策略下起草自然语言账单；(2)基于Transformer的预测器，提供日前消费估计及校准分位数区间；(3)碳排放核算模块；(4)负荷调度模块。

Result: 框架集成了四种生产级能力，具体实验结果未在摘要中详述。

Conclusion: 该框架为配电公司提供了统一的解决方案，同时满足可读账单、碳排放追踪和电网约束调度三大需求。

Abstract: Distribution utilities are now expected to deliver bills that customers can actually read attach a defensible carbon number to every kWh sold and schedule load against grid stress and emissions constraints We propose an end-to-end framework that unifies four production-grade capabilities under one architectural roof a generative-AI agent that drafts each customers natural-language billing statement from structured numeric inputs under a constrained decoding policy a transformer-based forecaster that supplies the day-ahead consumption estimate with calibrated quantile bands

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [PhysBrain 1.0 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15298)
*Shijie Lian,Bin Yu,Xiaopeng Lin,Changti Wu,Hang Yuan,Xiaolin Hu,Zhaolong Shen,Yuzhuo Miao,Haishan Liu,Yuxuan Tian,Yukun Shi,Cong Huang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysBrain 1.0通过从大规模人类日常视频中提取结构化物理常识知识，并将其迁移到视觉-语言-动作模型中，在多项多模态QA和具身控制基准上取得了SOTA结果，尤其是在跨域泛化方面表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人轨迹数据覆盖有限，难以学习广泛的物理理解能力；探索利用大规模人类日常视频作为替代数据源来获取结构化物理常识知识。

Method: 构建数据引擎从人类日常视频中提取场景元素、空间动态、动作执行和深度感知关系，生成问答监督数据训练PhysBrain VLM；再通过保留能力和语言敏感的自适应设计将物理先验迁移到VLA策略中。

Result: 在ERQA、PhysBench、SimplerEnv-WidowX、LIBERO、RoboCasa等多项基准上达到SOTA，在SimplerEnv上展现出极强的跨域泛化性能。

Conclusion: 从人类交互视频中规模化学习物理常识能够有效桥接多模态理解与机器人动作执行。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models have advanced rapidly, but robot trajectories alone provide limited coverage for learning broad physical understanding. PhysBrain 1.0 studies a complementary route: converting large-scale human egocentric video into structured physical commonsense supervision before robot adaptation. Our data engine extracts scene elements, spatial dynamics, action execution, and depth-aware relations, then turns them into question-answer supervision for training PhysBrain VLMs. The resulting physical priors are further transferred to VLA policies through a capability-preserving and language-sensitive adaptation design. Across multimodal QA benchmarks and embodied control benchmarks, including ERQA, PhysBench, SimplerEnv-WidowX, LIBERO, and RoboCasa, PhysBrain 1.0 achieves SOTA results and shows especially strong out-of-domain performance on SimplerEnv. These results suggest that scaling physical commonsense from human interaction video can provide an effective bridge from multimodal understanding to robot action.

</details>


### [130] [HoloMotion-1 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15336)
*Maiyue Chen,Kaihui Wang,Bo Zhang,Xihan Ma,Zhiyuan Yang,Yi Ren,Qijun Huang,Zihao Zhu,Yucheng Wang,Zhizhong Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HoloMotion-1是一个基于大规模混合运动数据集训练的仿人运动基础模型，通过结合视频重建运动、动作捕捉和自采数据，实现零样本全身运动跟踪，并可直接迁移到真实人形机器人上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法依赖动作捕捉数据，受限于数据规模和多样性，无法泛化到真实世界的多样化运动场景。作者希望通过构建大规模混合运动语料库来突破这一瓶颈，实现更鲁棒的全身运动跟踪。

Method: 1) 构建大规模混合运动语料库，融合视频重建运动（主要多样性来源）、动作捕捉数据（高保真监督）和自采数据（部署导向覆盖）；2) 采用大容量时间建模；3) 稀疏激活的MoE Transformer架构并配合KV-cache推理实现实时控制；4) 序列级训练策略提升长序列学习效率。

Result: 在多个未见运动基准上，HoloMotion-1鲁棒泛化到多样的运动类型和捕捉条件，显著优于先前方法的跟踪精度，且无需微调即可直接迁移到真实人形机器人。

Conclusion: 通过大规模混合数据训练和一系列解决异构数据挑战的技术设计，HoloMotion-1实现了超越传统MoCap-only训练的运动跟踪能力，验证了数据规模与多样性在仿人运动控制中的关键作用。

Abstract: In this report, we present HoloMotion-1, a humanoid motion foundation model for zero-shot whole-body motion tracking. A key innovation of HoloMotion-1 is to scale control-policy training with a large-scale hybrid motion corpus, where video-reconstructed motions from in-the-wild videos provide the dominant source of motion diversity, while curated motion-capture and in-house motion data provide higher-fidelity supervision and deployment-oriented coverage. This data regime enables HoloMotion-1 to move beyond conventional MoCap-only training and exposes the policy to substantially broader behaviors, capture conditions, and motion styles.
  Learning from such heterogeneous data introduces new challenges, including reconstruction noise, source-domain mismatch, uneven motion quality, and the need for temporal modeling under large behavioral variation. To address these challenges, HoloMotion-1 integrates large-capacity temporal modeling, a sparsely activated Mixture-of-Experts Transformer with KV-cache inference for real-time control, and a sequence-level training strategy that improves learning efficiency on extended motion sequences. Extensive experiments on multiple unseen motion benchmarks show that HoloMotion-1 generalizes robustly across diverse motion types and capture conditions, significantly improves tracking accuracy over prior methods, and transfers directly to a real humanoid robot without task-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [131] [Diffusion Policy for Coordinated Control of a Nonholonomic Mobile Base and Dual Arms in Door Opening and Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15352)
*Shangqun Yu,Matthew En,Daniel Wu,Sangjun Park,Ziyi Zhou,Seyed Fakoorian,Donghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于扩散模型的视觉运动控制策略，实现机器人双臂协调开阻尼拉门并穿过的长时域任务，在成功率和鲁棒性上优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重型自关门（尤其是拉门）的开启和通过是机器人领域的长期挑战，传统基于状态机的方法依赖手工设计轨迹，泛化性差且缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 采用基于扩散模型的视觉运动控制策略（diffusion based visuomotor control policy），实现非完整移动基座与双臂协调的端到端长时域控制。

Result: 单一路径策略在开启并穿过阻尼拉门上取得高成功率，并展现出对外部干扰的强鲁棒性，这是传统方法难以实现的。

Conclusion: 端到端扩散策略可学习需要操作与移动紧密协调的长时域任务，为机器人处理复杂约束下的物理交互提供了可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Opening heavy, self closing doors, especially those that require pulling remains a long standing challenge in robotics. Humans naturally employ both arms in a dexterous manner, rotating the handle, widening the gap, holding the door, switching arms when needed, and moving through while maintaining clearance. To replicate such behaviors, a robot must perform a long sequence of motions spanning multiple stages and interactions with different parts of the door. Traditional approaches rely on state machines that transition between manually defined stages (e.g., pulling after the knob is rotated, passing after the gap is sufficiently wide). While intuitive, these methods lack robustness, as hand crafted trajectories fail to generalize to the diversity of real world conditions without extensive engineering effort. Recent advances in imitation learning offer a scalable alternative, yet no existing visual action model has demonstrated simultaneous coordination of a nonholonomic base and dual arms for the complete door opening and passing task. In this paper, we tackle this complex, highly constrained problem using a diffusion based visuomotor control policy. Our results demonstrate that a single end to end policy can be learned to execute long horizon tasks requiring tight coordination between manipulation and locomotion. The resulting policy not only achieves a high success rate in opening and traversing damped pull doors but also demonstrates strong robustness to external disturbances capabilities that are difficult to realize with traditional methods.

</details>


### [132] [Where to Perch in a Tree: Vision-Guidance for Tree-Grasping Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15430)
*Alex Dunnett,Leonie Bottomley,Mirko Kovac,Basaran Bahadir Kocer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种用于视觉引导的自主树上栖息无人机的理想栖息位置定位方法。通过集成机器学习、图像分割和形态学处理算法评估树木分支的形状与结构，基于分支宽度、倾角和曲率等因素判断其适宜性。在超过10,000张城市树木图像的数据集上，该方法对76%的可行目标生成了成功结果，为后续融合深度感知和姿态传感数据的泛化方法奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常仅识别最近的可用分支，缺乏对分支质量（如宽度、坡度、曲率）的综合评估。本研究旨在构建一个更精细的视觉评估框架，使无人机能够自主选择结构上最适合栖息的树枝位置，提升自主栖息的可靠性和实用性。

Method: 集成机器学习、图像分割和二值图像形态学等多种图像处理算法；对每个分支评估三个关键因子：分支宽度、倾角（与水平面的夹角）和曲率；利用超过10,000张亚热带和温带季风气候下（2月至10月拍摄）的城市树木图像数据集进行验证。

Result: 在可行目标（分支直径足够粗且可用栖息空间至少等于肌腱驱动抓爪宽度的树木）中，方法成功率为76%。

Conclusion: 初步结果验证了方法的有效性，为未来的泛化方法奠定了基础；后续需融合深度感知传感器和姿态传感器数据以增强分支评估能力，并开发一系列改进功能和附加特性。

Abstract: This study demonstrates a method to locate an ideal perch location on a tree for vision-guided autonomous tree-perching drones. Various image processing algorithms, including those used for machine learning, image segmentation and binary image morphology, are implemented to assess the shape and structure of a tree. Rather than identifying the closest available branch, this study builds on vision methods by evaluating the potential of each branch, determining its suitability for perching based on factors such as branch width, slope (angle to the horizontal) and curvature. For a given tree-perching drone and a dataset of more than 10,000 urban tree images taken from February to October in a subtropical and temperate monsoon climate, the proposed method successfully produces a result for 76% of feasible targets. A feasible target defined as a tree where the branch diameters are sufficiently thick and where the available perching space is at least equal to the width of a tendon-driven grasping claw. These successful preliminary results create a foundation from which a number of identified improvements and additional features can be developed to create a generalised method; this will involve the incorporation of supplementary data from depth perception and attitude sensors to enhance the branch assessment.

</details>


### [133] [FLASH: Efficient Visuomotor Policy via Sparse Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15492)
*Jiaqi Bai,Jindou Jia,Yuxuan Hu,Gen Li,Xiangyu Chen,Tuo An,Kuangji Zuo,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLASH Policy提出了一种基于勒让德多项式轨迹表示和稀疏历史锚点流匹配的视觉运动策略，通过单步推理实现快速、高成功率的机器人操控，速度比扩散策略快175倍，比流匹配策略快18倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型和流匹配模型用于视觉运动策略时，迭代去噪过程导致高推理延迟，无法满足机器人实时控制需求。

Method: 1) 用连续勒让德多项式轨迹表示替代离散动作块生成，结合稀疏时间采样拟合专家演示以扩展动作时域；2) 从历史多项式系数而非高斯噪声初始化流匹配过程，缩短传输距离，实现精确单步推理；3) 解析多项式微分可直接为扭矩控制器提供速度前馈信号，无需数值近似。

Result: 在5个仿真和2个真实世界操控任务中，成功率达92%以上；单次推理时间31.40ms（比扩散策略快175倍，比流匹配策略快18倍）；训练收敛速度比ACT快4倍；控制器跟踪误差比离散动作基线降低5-7倍。

Conclusion: FLASH Policy在保持高成功率的同时，大幅降低了推理延迟和训练时间，并提高了控制精度，适合实时机器人操控应用。

Abstract: Generative models such as diffusion and flow matching have become dominant paradigms for visuomotor policy learning, yet their reliance on iterative denoising incurs high inference latency incompatible with real-time robotic control. We present Fast Legendre-polynomial Action policy via Sparse History-anchored flow (FLASH Policy), which replaces discrete action-chunk generation with continuous Legendre polynomial trajectory representation. Specifically, by fitting expert demonstrations under sparse temporal sampling, FLASH enables a single inference to cover a significantly extended action horizon. To further accelerate generation, FLASH initiates the flow matching process from history polynomial coefficients rather than uninformative Gaussian noise, shortening the transport distance and enabling accurate single-step inference. Moreover, analytic polynomial differentiation directly provides desired velocity feed-forward signals to the torque controller without numerical approximation. Extensive experiments on five simulated and two real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that FLASH achieves state-of-the-art success rates ($\ge 92\%$ across all tasks), a per-episode inference time of $31.40\,ms$ (up to $175\times$ faster than diffusion policies and $18\times$ faster than prior flow matching policies), up to $4\times$ faster training convergence than ACT, and $5\times$ to $7\times$ reduction in controller tracking error compared to discrete-action baselines.

</details>


### [134] [LAPS: Improving Incremental LiDAR Mapping using Active Pooling and Sampling for Neural Distance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15496)
*Dongjae Lee,Wooseong Yang,Yifu Tao,Maurice Fallon,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LAPS是一种针对增量神经建图的回放管理框架，通过基于可靠性的主动池化保留可靠历史样本，以及基于不确定性的主动采样聚焦欠约束区域，有效缓解了神经距离场在增量LiDAR建图中的灾难性遗忘问题，在多个基准上提升了重建完整性和几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经距离场在增量LiDAR建图中存在灾难性遗忘问题，新观测数据会破坏先前重建的几何结构。现有基于回放的训练方法依赖被动回放缓冲区和均匀采样，浪费内存于冗余观测，且对欠约束区域训练不足。

Method: 提出LAPS框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于可靠性的主动池化，在有限内存下保留可靠的历史样本；(2) 基于不确定性的主动采样，在在线更新中聚焦于欠约束区域进行优化。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准上，LAPS一致提升了重建完整性并保持了有竞争力的几何精度。在Oxford Spires的Blenheim Palace 05序列上，相比PIN-SLAM，召回率提升4.66个百分点，F1分数提升3.79个百分点。

Conclusion: LAPS通过主动回放管理有效改善了增量神经建图的性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Neural distance fields offer a compact and continuous representation of 3D geometry, making them attractive for incremental LiDAR mapping. However, their online optimization is vulnerable to catastrophic forgetting, where new observations can degrade previously reconstructed geometry. Replay-based training is commonly used to address this issue, but existing methods typically rely on passive replay buffers and uniform sampling, which can waste memory on redundant observations and under-train poorly constrained regions. We propose LAPS, a replay management framework for incremental neural mapping that improves both replay retention and replay allocation during online updates. LAPS combines reliability-based active pooling to retain reliable historical samples under limited memory with uncertainty-guided active sampling to focus optimization on under-constrained regions. Experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that LAPS consistently improves reconstruction completeness while maintaining competitive geometric accuracy. On Oxford Spires, it improves recall by 4.66 pp and F1-score by 3.79 pp over PIN-SLAM on the Blenheim Palace 05 sequence. We release our open source implementation at: https://github.com/dongjae0107/LAPS.

</details>


### [135] [A QUBO Formulation Framework for Kinematic Structure-Based Robot Design Optimization: A Robotic Hand Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15510)
*HyoJae Kang,Yeong Jae Park,Jeongdo Ahn,Dongil Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于二次无约束二元优化（QUBO）的机器人设计优化框架，利用运动学结构级评估指标，通过量子退火实现组合选择问题的求解，并以机器人手部设计作为案例验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人设计优化中，运动学结构层面的组合选择问题（如手指配置）具有高维离散特性，传统方法难以高效求解；同时量子退火等新兴计算范式需要与工程设计问题适配的数学表达形式。

Method: 将设计依赖指标通过经典计算评估，再将组合选择问题建模为QUBO形式，统一纳入个体设计奖励、重叠工作空间交互项、one-hot约束和结构依赖惩罚项；采用模拟退火作为基线验证，并在量子退火硬件上执行求解。

Result: 成功获得了满足one-hot选择和成对约束的可行设计组合，随着读取次数增加，目标函数值范围变窄；验证了QUBO框架在量子退火硬件上的适用性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种将运动学结构级机器人设计问题转化为组合优化问题的通用方法，可推广至其他机器人系统。

Abstract: This paper presents a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization-based formulation framework for robot design optimization using kinematic structure-level evaluation metrics. In the proposed framework, classical computation is used to evaluate design-dependent metrics while the resulting combinatorial selection problem is formulated in a structure compatible with quantum annealing-based optimization. A robotic hand is adopted as a representative case study, as its performance is determined by both the individual kinematic characteristics of each finger and interaction terms. The proposed formulation incorporates individual design rewards, overlap workspace interactions, one-hot constraint, and structural dependency penalties into a unified quadratic model. A 27-variable robotic hand design problem is constructed, and simulated annealing is used as a classical baseline to verify the feasibility of the formulation. Quantum annealing is further performed to examine the applicability of the proposed formulation to annealing-based hardware execution. The results show that feasible design combinations satisfying both one-hot selection and pairwise constraints can be obtained, with the observed objective-value range becoming narrower as the number of reads increases. In addition, the formulation process is discussed for other robotic systems. The proposed framework provides a generalized approach for transforming kinematic structure-based robot design problems into combinatorial optimization problems.

</details>


### [136] [Terrain Consistent Reference-Guided RL for Humanoid Navigation Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15517)
*William D. Compton,Zachary Olkin,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于参考轨迹引导的强化学习人形机器人行走策略，通过在训练中根据地形几何调制参考轨迹，并结合标准导航框架，实现了在复杂户外地形（含粗糙地面和多段楼梯）上超过70米的自主导航，所有感知与计算均机载完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人强化学习行走策略在复杂地形上的泛化能力有限，且难以与标准的导航规划框架无缝集成。需要一种方法既能利用参考轨迹引导学习，又能使轨迹适应地形变化，同时保持与现有导航基础设施的兼容性。

Method: 在强化学习训练循环内合成SE(2)可控的参考轨迹：将期望脚步投影到有效落脚点，并调整摆动脚和质心轨迹以适应地形。策略暴露出干净的SE(2)速度接口，与标准导航规划器兼容。使用MPC+控制障碍函数规划器进行硬件集成。

Result: 仿真中，环境条件化的参考轨迹相比环境无关的参考轨迹显著提升了参考跟踪性能。在硬件上，Unitree G1人形机器人在户外粗糙地形和多段楼梯上实现了超过70米的闭环自主导航。

Conclusion: 该方法有效融合了参考轨迹引导强化学习与地形感知，并通过标准SE(2)接口实现了与导航规划器的良好兼容，在复杂真实场景中验证了长距离自主导航的可行性。

Abstract: We present a method for training reference-guided, perceptive reinforcement learning locomotion policies for humanoid robots in which reference trajectories are modulated in training to be consistent with terrain geometry. Aiming to deploy our method with standard navigation autonomy infrastructure, we synthesize SE(2)-controllable reference trajectories inside the RL training loop, projecting desired footsteps onto valid footholds and adjusting swing-foot and center-of-mass trajectories to match the terrain. The resulting policy exposes a clean SE(2) velocity interface compatible with standard navigation planners. In simulation, environmentally-conditioned references significantly improve reference tracking performance compared to environment agnostic references. On hardware, we integrate the policy with an MPC + control barrier function planner and demonstrate long-horizon (>70m) closed-loop autonomous navigation on the Unitree G1 through outdoor environments containing rough terrain and consecutive flights of stairs, with all sensing and computation onboard.

</details>


### [137] [Wind-Aware Optimal Trajectory Planning for Efficient Gliding of Fixed-Wing Aerial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15619)
*Luca Morando,Nishanth Bobbili,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于伯恩斯坦多项式的非线性多代价轨迹规划器，用于小型固定翼无人机滑翔飞行，通过差分平坦性映射控制指令，并集成模拟净空率变差仪实现能量平衡，在CFD仿真和真实实验中验证了其抗风扰和避障能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统总能量控制系统（TECS）在滑翔飞行中需要精细调参和配平条件知识，且属于被动调节方式，难以在风扰和障碍约束下实现精确能量管理。

Method: 将能量调节从控制层提升到规划层，使用伯恩斯坦多项式生成C³连续轨迹，通过差分平坦性映射为控制指令；集成模拟净空率变差仪估计空气质量运动，约束滑翔至能量平衡状态；基于Dubins路径初始化巡航段轨迹，实现混合动力与无动力飞行衔接；在线重规划以匹配实验下沉极曲线。

Result: 在CFD仿真和真实固定翼平台上验证，能够在风扰和障碍物存在条件下可靠稳定下沉率、空速和滑翔比。

Conclusion: 将能量管理从反应式控制转移到规划层面的方法是有效的，能够实现更鲁棒的滑翔能量调节，支持混合任务模式。

Abstract: Gliding offers small fixed-wing UAVs extended endurance and silent operation but requires accurate energy management, especially under wind disturbances and obstacle constraints. Traditional Total Energy Control Systems based controllers regulate the trade between potential and kinetic energy reactively, often requiring fine-tuning and trim-conditions knowledge. In this work, we shift the regulation to the planning level and present a nonlinear, multi-cost trajectory planner for small UAV gliders. The method generates $\mathcal{C}^3$ continuous trajectories based on Bernstein polynomials, mapped into control commands through differential flatness, and re-planned online to match experimentally derived sink polar curves. A simulated netto variometer is integrated into the optimization to estimate air mass motion, constraining the glide to energy-balanced states. Consecutive gliding trajectories are linked by cruising segments computed through trajectories initialized on Dubins path-based waypoints, enabling hybrid missions that combine powered and unpowered flight. The approach is validated in CFD simulations and real-world experiments with a fixed-wing platform, showing reliable stabilization of sink rate, airspeed, and glide ratio under wind gusts and in presence of obstacles.

</details>


### [138] [Propagating Unsafe Actions in LLM Controlled Multi-Robot Collaboration via Single Robot Compromise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15641)
*Zhen Huang,Zhihuang Liu,Mengxuan Luo,Weishang Wu,Zhiping Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对多机器人LLM规划器的攻击范式，攻击者仅需控制单个机器人的入口，通过机器人间通信传播恶意指令，导致系统协同执行危险动作。实验表明该攻击具有高服从性（1.00）、快速传播（3轮感染全部机器人）和高隐蔽性（0.81），揭示了多机器人规划器中存在的安全对齐缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注单机器人场景下的LLM安全威胁，而多机器人协作场景中通过机器人间通信传播的安全风险尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出InfectBot攻击范式：攻击者仅与单个入口机器人交互，被入侵的机器人通过同侪通信将恶意意图传播至整个多机器人系统，实现协调的 unsafe 行为。

Result: 在失职、隐私泄露和公共安全危害三个高风险维度上，实验显示：服从性最高达1.00，感染性达0.90，仅需3.0轮即可感染所有机器人，且隐蔽性维持在0.81。

Conclusion: 多机器人LLM规划器存在持久的安全对齐缺陷，协调机制可能无意中允许对抗性指令覆盖安全要求，尤其在紧急或权利冲突等需要权衡的临界情况下风险加剧。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as general planners in embodied intelligence, enabling high level coordination and low level task planning for both single robot and multi-robot collaboration. This increasing reliance on embodied LLM planners also raises critical security concerns, since misaligned or manipulated instructions can be translated into physical actions. Prior work has studied such threats in single robot settings, while security risks in LLM controlled multi-robot collaboration, especially those propagated through inter robot communication, remain largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel attack paradigm for multi-robot system in which the adversary interacts with only a single entry robot. The compromised robot then propagates malicious intent through peer communication, leading to coordinated unsafe actions across the system. Our evaluation, covering high risk dimensions of dereliction of duty, privacy compromise, and public safety hazards, reveals a persistent safety alignment gap in multi-robot planners. We quantify this process with three metrics, obedience, infectiousness, and stealthiness. Experiments demonstrate both persistent attacker control and rapid propagation: obedience reaches 1.00 in the strongest cases, and infectiousness rises to 0.90. Notably, the attack is highly efficient, requiring as few as 3.0 rounds to compromise all the robots while maintaining a stealthiness score of 0.81. Such risks are amplified when robots must resolve trade offs in critical situations, such as emergencies or conflicts of rights, because the coordination mechanism can unintentionally allow adversarial instructions to override safety requirements. The code is available at https://github.com/TheFatInsect/InfectBot.

</details>


### [139] [MyoChallenge 2025: A New Benchmark for Human Athletic Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15650)
*Cheryl Wang,Chun Kwang Tan,Balint K. Hodossy,Eric Lyu,Jun Guo,Wentao Zhao,Huaping Liu,Chengkun Li,Merkourios Simos,Bianca Ziliotto,Alexander Mathis,Siyuan Liu,Jiahao Chen,Shanlin Zhong,Bo Jiang,Ci Song,Yaoye Zhu,Chenhui Zuo,Yanan Sui,Mohamed Irfan Refai,Massimo Sartori,Guillaume Durandau,Vikash Kumar,Vittorio Caggiano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MyoChallenge at NeurIPS 2025 建立了一个基于高保真肌肉骨骼模型和物理仿真的运动控制智能基准平台，通过乒乓对打和足球罚球两个赛道，吸引了近70支团队和超过560份投稿，推动了结合机器学习与生物力学的运动控制算法发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI和机器人系统难以复现人类运动智能中快速决策、精准控制、敏捷性和协调执行的无缝结合，同时由于物理设备限制，复杂肌肉协调的体内测量十分困难，亟需一个可重复、标准化的基准来弥合这一理解鸿沟。

Method: 利用MyoSuite开源框架，构建高保真肌肉骨骼模型并在物理仿真环境中结合机器学习算法，设置两个赛道：上肢控制的乒乓球对打任务（含手臂、手和躯干）和下肢控制的足球罚球任务（含腿和躯干）。参赛者采用基于物理的运动规划器、在线策略行为克隆、层次化规划和肌肉协同等技术。

Result: 吸引了近70支团队和超过560份全球投稿，汇聚了医生、神经科学家和机器学习专家等多元社区，开发出了多项面向肌肉骨骼系统运动敏捷性的先进控制算法。

Conclusion: MyoChallenge'25 通过将标准化任务和生理真实模型整合到开源框架中，为机器学习、生物力学、运动科学和神经科学领域的跨学科研究提供了一个可复现、可重用的试验平台。

Abstract: Athletic performance represents the pinnacle of human motor intelligence, demanding rapid choices, precise control, agility, and coordinated physical execution. Replicating this seamless combination of capabilities remains elusive in current artificial intelligence and robotic systems. Concurrently, understanding the biological mastery of these movements is hindered because complex muscle coordination is rarely measured in vivo due to the limitations of physical equipment. To bridge this fundamental gap in understanding, MyoChallenge at NeurIPS 2025 established a pioneering benchmark for motor control intelligence in sports, leveraging high-fidelity musculoskeletal models within physics simulation combined with machine learning-driven algorithms. The competition introduces two distinct tracks emphasizing either upper or lower limbs control: a table tennis rally task utilizing a biomechanic upper limb composed of an arm with a hand and a trunk; and a soccer penalty kick using a biomechanic model of legs and a trunk. Marking the fourth iteration of the MyoChallenge series, this event attracted almost 70 teams and over 560 submissions globally, uniting a diverse community ranging from physicians and neuroscientists to machine learning experts. The competition facilitated the development of several state-of-the-art control algorithms for a musculoskeletal system capable of sports agility, leveraging techniques such as physics-based motion planners, on-policy behaviour cloning, hierarchical planning, and muscle synergies. By integrating standardized tasks and physiologically realistic models into the open-source framework of MyoSuite, MyoChallenge'25 serves as a reproducible and reusable testbed to accelerate interdisciplinary research across machine learning, biomechanics, sports science, and neuroscience. Project page: https://www.myosuite.org//myochallenge/myochallenge-2025.

</details>


### [140] [PCASim: Promptable Closed-loop Adversarial Simulation for Urban Traffic Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15654)
*Chuancheng Zhang,Zhenhao Wang,Kaizheng Li,Yaran Lin,Qiang Guo,Bin Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个闭环测试框架PCASim，通过构建对抗行为知识库、结合大语言模型生成安全关键场景，并利用强化学习训练车辆行为，实现对抗场景生成与安全智能体训练的协同进化，在语言生成准确率、场景转换成功率和避障能力上分别提升12%、8%和30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究很少探索将对抗场景生成与安全智能体训练在闭环测试中集成，以实现两者的协同进化和相互增强，尤其在包含大量边缘案例的城市自动驾驶环境。

Method: 1) 对开源数据集应用基于规则的过滤构建对抗行为知识库；2) 结合面向仿真环境的知识检索模块；3) 使用大语言模型整合知识驱动、数据驱动和对抗驱动方法生成安全关键交通场景；4) 采用强化学习模型训练不同车辆类型的行为以丰富场景多样性。

Result: 领域特定语言生成准确率提升12%，新生成场景转换成功率提升8%，避障能力增强30%。

Conclusion: 提出的PCASim框架能有效实现对抗场景生成与安全智能体训练的协同进化，同时保持场景真实感，显著提升自动驾驶闭环测试的多项性能指标。

Abstract: Real-world autonomous driving, particularly in urban environments with numerous corner cases, requires rigorous testing to ensure product safety and robustness. However, few studies have explored integrating adversarial scenario generation with the training of safety agents in closed-loop testing, enabling efficient co-evolution and mutual enhancement of both. To address this challenge, an adversarial behavior knowledge repository is constructed by applying rule-based filtering to an open-source dataset, combined with knowledge retrieval modules tailored for simulation environments. A large language model (LLM) is employed to integrate knowledge-, data-, and adversarial-driven approaches, generating safety-critical traffic scenarios customized to user needs. Additionally, while evaluating the generated scenarios, we employ reinforcement learning models to train the behaviors of different types of vehicles, thereby enriching scenario diversity beyond existing datasets while preserving realism. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework improves the accuracy of domain-specific language generation by 12\%. Moreover, the success rate of newly generated scenario transformations increases by 8\%, while obstacle-avoidance capability is enhanced by 30\%. For the complete manuscript, please refer to: https://zhenhaooo.github.io/PCASim.github.io/

</details>


### [141] [Feedback World Model Enables Precise Guidance of Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15705)
*Tuo An,Jindou Jia,Gen Li,Jingliang Li,Chuhao Zhou,Pengfei Liu,Bofan Lyu,Jiaqi Bai,Xinying Guo,Geng Li,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出反馈世界模型（Feedback World Model），通过在推理时利用真实观测在线更新潜在状态来校正世界模型预测，无需额外训练数据或参数更新，在分布外场景下将预测误差降低76.4%，成功率提升30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在部署时遇到分布外状态会导致预测不可靠，而执行过程中机器人直接观测到的真实下一状态（预测与实际的差异）这一天然信号未被有效利用。

Method: 提出反馈世界模型范式：在推理时维护轻量级反馈状态，利用实时观测在线迭代校正未来预测；将其解释为潜在空间观测器并给出收敛保证；引入动作感知引导，强调动作可控分量、抑制无关变化。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus、Robomimic及真实操控任务上，分布偏移下预测误差最高降低76.4%，OOD成功率提升30%。

Conclusion: 在推理时融入实时反馈为静态世界建模提供了一种简单而强大的替代方案，有效提升了分布外场景下的预测精度与策略性能。

Abstract: World models aim to improve robotic decision making by predicting the consequences of actions. However, in practice, their predictions often become unreliable once the robot encounters states outside the training distribution, limiting their effectiveness at deployment. We observe that execution itself provides a natural but underutilized signal: after each action, the robot directly observes the true next state, revealing the mismatch between predicted and actual outcomes. Building on this insight, we propose feedback world model, a new paradigm that closes the loop between prediction and observation at inference time. Instead of treating the world model as a static open-loop predictor, our method maintains a lightweight feedback state that is updated online to iteratively correct future predictions, compensating for model errors using real-time observations without additional training data or parameter updates. We show that this process can be interpreted as a latent-space observer and admits convergence guarantees under mild conditions. We further introduce action-aware guidance to better translate corrected predictions into control by emphasizing action-controllable components while suppressing irrelevant variations. Experiments on LIBERO-Plus, Robomimic, and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that our method substantially improves both prediction accuracy and policy performance under distribution shift. In particular, it reduces world model prediction error by up to 76.4% and improves out-of-distribution (OOD) success rate by 30%. These results show that incorporating real-time feedback at inference time provides a simple yet powerful alternative to static world modeling.

</details>


### [142] [Learning Dynamic Pick-and-Place for a Legged Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15713)
*Moonkyu Jung,Jiseong Lee,Zhengmao He,Donghoon Youm,Juhyeok Mun,HyeongJun Kim,Hyunsik Oh,Donghyuk Choi,Jungwoo Hur,Jie Song,Jemin Hwangbo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种分层强化学习框架，使四足机器人配备6自由度机械臂能够在动态行走中完成抓取-放置任务，支持高达2.3kg负载，仿真成功率86.05%，实物实验平均成功率73.3%，执行时间4.06s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前腿式机械手在动态操作中难以同时实现精确操控与协调运动，尤其在较重负载和较大工作空间下表现不佳。

Method: 采用分层强化学习框架，结合显式质量估计模块实现自适应全身控制，支持不同重量物体的动态抓取与放置。

Result: 仿真中2.3kg负载成功率86.05%；实物实验中1.3kg负载在0-1.1m高度范围内平均成功率73.3%，平均执行时间4.06s。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了四足移动机械手在较重负载和扩展工作空间下的自适应全身动态抓取-放置，优于先前处理轻质物体且分段慢速运动的方法。

Abstract: Legged manipulators extend robotic capabilities beyond static manipulation by integrating agile locomotion with versatile arm control. However, achieving precise manipulation while maintaining coordinated locomotion remains a major challenge. This work presents a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework for dynamic pick-and-place tasks using a quadruped equipped with a 6-DOF robotic arm. The framework incorporates an explicit mass estimation module enabling adaptive whole-body control for objects with varying weights. In simulation, the system achieves an 86.05% success rate with payloads up to 2.3 kg. The approach is further validated through real-world experiments across six representative scenarios with controlled variations in object physical properties (size and mass) and task heights. Specifically, within a wide vertical workspace ranging from ground level to 1.1~m-high tabletops, the system demonstrates an average success rate of 73.3% for payloads up to 1.3 kg, with an average execution time of 4.06 s. Unlike prior works that handle lightweight objects and execute pick-and-place motions with slow, piecewise motions, the proposed framework exploits concurrent locomotion and manipulation for dynamic, continuous execution. These results demonstrate the potential of quadrupedal mobile manipulators for adaptive, whole-body pick-and-place with heavier payloads and extended workspaces.

</details>


### [143] [Hierarchical and Holistic Open-Vocabulary Functional 3D Scene Graphs for Indoor Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15753)
*Xinggang Hu,Chenyangguang Zhang,Alexandros Delitzas,Xiangkui Zhang,Marc Pollefeys,Francis Engelmann,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于2D视觉定位和3D图优化的开放词汇管线，用于构建包含密集桌面物体和多级功能关系的功能3D场景图，以解决小尺度、密集相似实例、缺乏视觉锚定、实例混淆和视角不确定性等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有功能3D场景图基准覆盖有限，管线设计过于简单，主要关注大型家具且缺乏层次结构，未能充分挖掘其在场景理解和机器人操作中的潜力。

Method: 提出开放词汇管线：1）基于2D视觉证据锚定细粒度功能边；2）利用多线索跨帧关联3D节点；3）将边关联建模为时序图优化，融合证据累积、熵正则化和时序平滑；4）执行全局层次整形恢复层次化图结构。

Result: 在具有挑战性的真实场景中，该方法能够可靠地推断功能3D场景图，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 通过扩展基准覆盖密集桌面物体和显式多级功能关系，并设计相应的优化管线，进一步释放了功能3D场景图在实用场景中的潜力。

Abstract: Functional 3D scene graphs offer a versatile and flexible representation for 3D scene understanding and robotic manipulation, defined by object nodes, interactive elements, and functional relationship edges. However, their potential remains underexplored due to the limited coverage of existing benchmarks and the overly straightforward design of previous pipelines, which primarily focus on large-scale furniture but lack of hierarchical structures. Therefore, in this work, we extend the benchmark coverage by introducing dense tabletop objects and explicit multi-level functional relationships. This expansion introduces critical challenges involving small-scale, dense, and similar instances, with lack of visual anchoring in relational reasoning, instance confusion during cross-frame fusion, and attribution uncertainty under dynamic viewpoints. To address these issues, we propose an open-vocabulary pipeline based on 2D visual grounding and 3D graph optimization. Specifically, we anchor fine-grained functional edges from 2D visual evidence, and associate nodes across frames in 3D using multiple cues. Furthermore, edge association is formulated as temporal graph optimization, integrating evidence accumulation, entropy regularization, and temporal smoothing to robustly determine the functional connections of each node. Finally, global hierarchy shaping is performed to recover the hierarchical graph structure. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed method can reliably infer functional 3D scene graphs in challenging real-world scenes, thereby further unlocking their potential for practical applications.

</details>


### [144] [Lamarckian Inheritance in Dynamic Environments: How Key Variables Affect Evolutionary Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15769)
*K. Ege de Bruin,Kyrre Glette,Kai Olav Ellefsen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人体脑协同优化中，拉马克式继承（将学习到的控制器参数传递给后代）相比达尔文式继承的优劣，发现其效果取决于环境变化的冲突性和可预测性：仅在变化既冲突又不可预测时表现较差，而添加环境传感器可恢复其优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器人形态与控制协同优化中的耦合挑战，澄清动态环境中拉马克式继承在进化机器人学中 contradictory 的证据，探究其有效性的关键条件。

Method: 将形态优化作为进化、控制器优化作为终身学习相结合，采用拉马克式继承传递学到的控制器参数；使用虚拟软体机器人，结合贝叶斯优化和强化学习两种学习方法，在动态环境中对比拉马克式与达尔文式继承的性能。

Result: 拉马克式继承仅在环境变化既冲突（对控制要求相反）又不可预测时表现劣于达尔文式继承；添加环境变化检测传感器可在冲突环境中恢复拉马克式继承的优势。

Conclusion: 拉马克式继承在动态环境中的有效性取决于环境变化的冲突性与可预测性两个变量，通过感知机制提高可预测性可充分发挥其优势，为机器人体脑协同进化提供了设计指导。

Abstract: The co-optimization of a robot's body and brain presents a coupled challenge: the morphology constrains which control strategies are effective, while the control determines how well the morphology performs. To address this, we combine morphology optimization as evolution with controller optimization as lifetime learning, utilizing Lamarckian inheritance to transfer learned controller parameters from parent to offspring. In dynamic environments, existing literature presents conflicting evidence: while traditional evolutionary theory often suggests Lamarckian inheritance lacks benefit, recent studies in evolutionary robotics indicate it can improve performance. We hypothesize that this is because previous works have not included all relevant variables with dynamic environments. In this work, we show that the benefit of Lamarckian inheritance depends on two variables: how conflicting the environmental changes are to robot control, and the predictability of those changes for the robotic agent. Using virtual soft robots and two different learning approaches, Bayesian optimization and reinforcement learning, we show that Lamarckian inheritance only underperforms Darwinian inheritance when the changes are both conflicting and unpredictable. We find that adding a sensor to detect environmental changes restores the benefits for Lamarckian inheritance in conflicting environments, by allowing robotic agents to predict the need for a different behavior, thereby generalizing their control.

</details>


### [145] [Reactive Robot-Centric Safety for Autonomous Navigation in Constrained and Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15782)
*Viswa Narayanan Sankaranarayanan,Vignesh K. Viswanathan,Akshit Saradagi,Sumeet Satpute,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅依靠机载传感器的实时安全控制架构，将3D LIDAR感知与复合控制屏障函数(CBF)安全滤波器集成到自主导航管线中，通过在四足机器人平台上的地下环境实地实验验证了其在动态障碍物、不安全参考指令、定位异常及狭窄通道等场景下的可靠运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自主机器人在空间受限的动态环境中仅依靠机载传感器实现实时安全导航的问题。

Method: 提出一种实时控制架构，将3D LIDAR感知的复合控制屏障函数(CBF)安全滤波器直接集成到自主导航管线中；安全区域定义为与平台几何一致的机体坐标系椭球体，通过时变CBF公式化处理因机器人旋转产生的世界坐标系下的时变约束。

Result: 在地下环境中利用四足机器人平台进行多项实地实验，验证了系统在动态障碍物、不安全高层参考指令、突发定位异常以及狭窄走廊等场景下的可靠运行能力。

Conclusion: 该感知驱动框架能够从机载点云数据动态强制执行碰撞避免约束，在控制频率下处理大量约束，同时对名义任务执行的干扰最小化，有效保障了机器人在复杂动态环境中的实时安全。

Abstract: In this work, we address the problem of ensuring real-time safety in autonomous robot navigation, in spatially constrained dynamic environments, by utilizing only onboard sensors. We present a real-time control architecture that integrates a 3D LIDAR perception-based composite control barrier function(CBF)-based safety filter directly into the autonomy pipeline. The proposed perception-driven framework enforces collision avoidance constraints dynamically from onboard point cloud data, thus allowing a large number of constraints to be handled at the control frequency, while remaining minimally invasive to nominal task execution. The safety region is defined as an ellipsoid in the body-frame, consistent with the geometry of the platform, which induces time-varying constraints in the world frame as the robot rotates; this effect is handled through a dedicated formulation of time-varying (CBF) for each LIDAR point. We validate the system through multiple field experiments in underground environments by utilizing a quadruped platform performing a visual inspection task, demonstrating reliable operation in the presence of dynamic obstacles, unsafe high-level references, abrupt localization anomalies, and while traversing through narrow corridors.

</details>


### [146] [Structured Jacobian Construction for Motion Optimization with High-Order Time Derivatives in Multi-Link Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15845)
*Taiki Ishigaki,Ko Ayusawa,Eiichi Yoshida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多连杆系统结构的高阶时间导数雅可比计算框架，通过解析推导运动学和动力学量（动量、力、关节力矩等）关于广义坐标及其高阶导数的雅可比矩阵，相较于数值微分和自动微分提高了计算效率与数值稳定性，并成功应用于直接和逆运动优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人或人体的运动优化中，代价函数常包含高阶时间导数（如 jerk、力的时间变化率）以捕捉平滑性和感知特征，但现有方法（数值微分或自动微分）未充分利用多连杆系统的底层结构，导致计算成本高且数值不稳定。

Method: 基于全面的运动计算框架，将物理量及其高阶时间导数沿多连杆结构系统表示，并解析推导运动学量（位置、速度、加速度、jerk）和动力学量（动量、力、关节力矩）关于广义坐标及其高阶导数的雅可比矩阵，适用于直接优化和逆优化。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提方法相比数值微分和自动微分在计算效率上显著提升，同时保持相当的精度；在逆优化任务中，能够从运动数据中有效恢复代价函数权重。

Conclusion: 该形式化为涉及高阶时间导数的多连杆系统运动优化提供了一种可扩展的、结构化的计算基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel framework for Jacobian computation in motion optimization problems involving multi-link systems, where physical quantities are represented using higher-order time derivatives. In motion optimization of robots and humans, cost functions may incorporate higher-order time derivatives, such as jerk or the time variation of forces, to capture smoothness and perceptual characteristics, particularly in motion skill analysis and expressive behaviors, thereby necessitating Jacobian computations involving these quantities. However, such Jacobians are typically computed using numerical or automatic differentiation without explicitly exploiting the underlying multi-link structure, which can lead to increased computational cost and numerical instability. To address this limitation, we propose a structured Jacobian formulation for motion optimization, based on the comprehensive motion computation framework, in which physical quantities and their higher-order time derivatives are systematically represented along the multi-link structure. The proposed method systematically derives analytical expressions for Jacobians of kinematic and dynamic quantities, including momentum, forces, and joint torques, with respect to generalized coordinates and their higher-order derivatives. The resulting framework is applicable to both direct and inverse optimization. Through numerical experiments, we demonstrate that the proposed method improves computational efficiency compared to numerical and automatic differentiation, while achieving comparable accuracy. Furthermore, we demonstrate its effectiveness in inverse optimization by recovering cost function weights from motion data. Together, these results indicate that the proposed formulation provides a scalable and structured computational foundation for motion optimization involving higher-order time derivatives in multi-link systems.

</details>


### [147] [Designing for Robot Wranglers: A Synthesis of Literature and Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15892)
*David Porfirio,Ian McDermott,Hsin-Mei Chen,Satoru Satake,Takayuki Kanda,Thomas D. LaToza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过范围综述和自我反思，揭示了机器人操作员（robot wrangler）这一新兴角色的工作复杂性，并提出了支持其工作的设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人越来越多地进入人类空间，机器人操作员这一角色随之出现，但该角色的需求和工作特征尚未被充分理解和研究。

Method: 采用范围综述（scoping review）梳理文献中机器人操作的类型学，并结合研究者自身在该领域的第一手和想象经验进行反思分析。

Result: 发现"机器人操作"是一个涵盖高度复杂和多样化活动的总括性术语，这些活动的异质性使得该劳动难以被有效描述和支持。

Conclusion: 基于综述和反思，提出了一系列设计启示，旨在从个体层面和更广泛的服务生态系统层面支持机器人操作员。

Abstract: Robots are increasingly present in human spaces, such as for conducting deliveries in hospitals, interacting with visitors at museums, and stocking items in warehouses. To ensure the seamless integration of robots into these spaces, a new role in human-robot interaction is emerging - the robot wrangler, namely an individual who is responsible for setting up, overseeing, and troubleshooting the robot. To understand the needs of this stakeholder, we conducted a scoping review that uncovered a typology of robot wrangling across the research literature, and discovered that wrangling is an umbrella term that collapses a highly complex and heterogeneous space of activities, often rendering this labor difficult to characterize and support. To further clarify and understand robot wrangling, we then reflected on our own firsthand and imagined experiences as robot wranglers within our own respective domains. Guided by the scoping review and our reflections, we devise a series of design implications for supporting wranglers directly as individuals and as members of a wider service ecology.

</details>


### [148] [Dynamic Plasma Shape Control with Arbitrary Sensor Subsets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15935)
*D. Sorokin,M. Stokolesov,A. Granovskiy,I. Prokofyev,E. Adishchev,M. Nurgaliev,E. Khayrutdinov,G. Subbotin,R. Clark,D. Orlov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于强化学习的托卡马克等离子体形状控制方法，能够在传感器故障下同时实现形状跟踪与平衡重建，并在DIII-D实验装置上验证了零样本动态轨迹跟踪能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将平衡重建与线性控制器分离，且假设传感器完全正常工作，无法同时处理动态形状跟踪与传感器故障问题。

Method: 使用强化学习智能体在NSFsim高保真模拟器中训练，采用非对称Actor-Critic架构，Actor端配备辅助形状重建头，训练时随机屏蔽30%传感器模拟故障。

Result: 在静态配置下形状误差2.01 cm，零样本跟踪动态形状序列，单个策略适应任意传感器子集，成功迁移到DIII-D实验和GSevolve模拟器。

Conclusion: 强化学习方法可同时解决动态形状跟踪与传感器故障容错问题，具备实验迁移能力，为托卡马克等离子体控制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Plasma shape control in tokamaks requires a real-time controller that tracks dynamically changing shape targets while tolerating diagnostic failures. Classical approaches decompose the problem into equilibrium reconstruction followed by a linear controller, and assume a fixed, fully operational sensor set. We present a reinforcement learning agent that addresses both limitations simultaneously. The agent is trained in NSFsim, a high-fidelity tokamak simulator configured for DIII-D, on a curated dataset of 120 experimental plasma shapes. The shape targets are resampled as random step changes every 0.25 s, exposing the agent to diverse transitions across the full shape envelope. At test time the agent zero-shot tracks dynamic shape sequences; on a held-out static configuration in simulation it achieves a mean shape error of 2.01 cm, and dynamic trajectory following is demonstrated qualitatively in simulation and on the physical device. Diagnostic dropout randomly masks 30% of magnetic sensors per episode, yielding a single policy robust to arbitrary sensor subsets without backup controllers or mode-switching logic. An asymmetric actor-critic architecture with privileged equilibrium information improves value estimation under partial observability; an auxiliary shape reconstruction head on the actor enables end-to-end shape reconstruction from raw diagnostics and serves as an interpretability tool for policy analysis. The policy transfers to experimental DIII-D shots, where it directly commands the coil actuators on two dynamic shape maneuvers, and to the independent GSevolve simulator.

</details>


### [149] [FocalPolicy: Frequency-Optimized Chunking and Locally Anchored Flow Matching for Coherent Visuomotor Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15944)
*Qian He,Zhenshuo Yang,Wenqi Liang,Chunhui Hao,Nicu Sebe,Jiandong Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对现有策略在生成连贯长程动作时存在的跨片段不连续问题，提出FocalPolicy，通过频率优化分块与局部锚定流匹配实现精度与远见的协同平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视动策略通常侧重于优化块内动作分布，忽略了块间连贯性，导致跨片段不连续，阻碍了长程动作的学习。

Method: 提出FocalPolicy，包含两个核心：1) 远见复合目标：对近端动作进行时域对齐监督，同时对多个未来动作块进行频域结构正则化以提升跨块连贯性；2) 局部锚定采样：在一致性流匹配训练中增强目标信号传播效率。

Result: 大量实验表明FocalPolicy优于现有方法，且所提模块具有良好的泛化性，可迁移至其他基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过频域正则化与局部锚定流匹配，有效解决了跨块不连续问题，实现了精度与远见的平衡，验证了方法的有效性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies aim to learn complex manipulation tasks from expert demonstrations. However, generating smooth and coherent trajectories remains challenging, as it requires balancing proximal precision with distal foresight. Existing approaches typically focus on optimizing intra-chunk action distributions, often neglecting the inter-chunk coherence. Consequently, inter-chunk discontinuities significantly impede the learning of coherent long-horizon actions. To overcome this limitation and achieve a synergetic balance between precision and foresight, we propose FocalPolicy, a foresight-aware visuomotor policy that combines Frequency-Optimized Chunking with Locally Anchored flow matching. We introduce a foresight composite objective that supervises time-domain alignment within the proximal actions while regularizing frequency-domain structure over multiple future action chunks to improve cross-chunk coherence. To efficiently learn complex action distributions, we design locally anchored campling to enhance target signal propagation efficiency during consistency flow matching training.
