Extensive experiments demonstrate that FocalPolicy outperforms existing approaches and confirm the generalizability of our modules to other baselines. Project website: https://focalpolicy.github.io/

</details>


### [150] [A Reproducible and Physically Feasible Dynamic Parameter Identification Framework for a Low-Cost Robot Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15949)
*Junji Oaki,Koki Yamane,Koki Inami,Sho Sakaino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对低成本机器人臂CRANE-X7，提出了一套结合物理可行性恢复与统计学中心性筛选的动态参数辨识框架，通过去除惯性积、结构化轨迹设计、SDP投影和CLIE优化，最终获得统计一致且物理可行的动力学模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本机器人平台（如CRANE-X7）的动态参数辨识面临物理可行性差、参数可辨识性低等问题，现有方法难以保证辨识结果既统计合理又物理可接受。

Method: 1) 根据近似连杆对称性移除惯性积，将刚体模型从65个参数简化为39个基参数；2) 设计结构化单关节和相邻关节运动基元作为辨识轨迹；3) 采用预处理→逆动力学回归OLS→条件SDP投影（恢复物理可行性）→闭环输入误差（CLIE）精化的流水线；4) 在PCA空间中分析40条结构化轨迹的候选解，选取统计中心的代表性模型；5) 通过全位姿惯性矩阵正定性审核筛选，必要时通过局部后CLIE SDP修正步骤校正。

Result: 参数云从OLS到SDP再到CLIE逐步收敛集中；最终选定的模型在留出验证运动上保持高预测精度，验证了统计一致且物理可行的动力学模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为低成本机器人平台提供了一条实用路径，能够获得统计上连贯且物理上可行的动态模型。

Abstract: This paper presents a reproducible and physically feasible dynamic parameter identification framework for CRANE-X7, a low-cost robot arm driven by modular smart actuators. To improve practical identifiability, products of inertia are removed according to approximate link symmetry, reducing the rigid-body model from 65 to 39 base parameters. Identification motions are hand-designed from structured single-joint and adjacent-joint primitives under practical joint-range limits. The proposed pipeline combines preprocessing, inverse-dynamics-regressor-based ordinary least squares (OLS), conditional semidefinite-programming (SDP) projection for feasibility recovery, and closed-loop input error (CLIE) refinement. Candidate solutions from 40 structured trajectories are analyzed in a common PCA space to select a statistically central representative model. Because statistical centrality alone does not ensure physical acceptability, the selected model is finally screened by an all-pose positive-definiteness audit of the inertia matrix and, when necessary, corrected by a localized post-CLIE SDP rescue step. Experiments show that the parameter cloud becomes progressively more concentrated from OLS to SDP and CLIE, while the final accepted model preserves high predictive accuracy on held-out validation motions. These results demonstrate a practical route to statistically coherent and physically feasible dynamic models for low-cost robot platforms.

</details>


### [151] [OHP-RL: Online Human Preference as Guidance in Reinforcement Learning for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15971)
*Yunyang Mo,Jian Li,Qiwei Wu,Yihang Kang,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OHP-RL框架，将人类干预视为偏好信息而非示范信号，通过状态依赖偏好门控自适应调节人类指导对策略学习的影响，在真实机器人接触式操作任务中实现更高效、更稳定且更符合人类偏好的策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL方法在真实世界部署中存在探索低效和不安全问题；人类干预虽被用作辅助信号，但未能充分捕捉干预中蕴含的关于何时及如何引导自主性的丰富信息。

Method: 提出OHP-RL框架，将人类干预建模为偏好信息（而非精确动作模仿）；引入状态依赖偏好门控机制，自适应调节人类干预对策略学习的介入时机和影响程度。

Result: 在Franka机器人的三个真实接触式操作任务中，OHP-RL在成功率、收敛速度、人类干预成本方面均优于现有方法，且学得策略更稳定、更符合人类行为。

Conclusion: 将人类干预作为偏好信息而非示范信号，结合自适应偏好门控机制，能够在间歇性不完美反馈下有效引导RL策略学习，降低人类负担同时提升学习性能和行为对齐度。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning (RL) enables robots to acquire skills autonomously, its real-world deployment is severely limited by inefficient and unsafe exploration. Human-in-the-loop interventions offer a practical solution, yet existing methods typically exploit these interventions as auxiliary training signals, without fully capturing the richer information they provide about when and how autonomy should be guided.
  Human interventions often encode relative preferences over behavior under safety and task constraints, rather than prescribing exact actions to imitate. Motivated by this perspective, we propose Online Human Preference as Guidance in Reinforcement Learning (OHP-RL), a framework that leverages human interventions as preference information to guide policy learning. OHP-RL introduces a state-dependent preference gate that adaptively regulates when and to what extent human interventions should shape policy learning. This design enables the agent to benefit from intermittent and imperfect human feedback while preserving autonomous exploration and stable policy optimization.
  We evaluate OHP-RL on three challenging real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks on a Franka robot. Across all tasks, OHP-RL consistently achieves strong success rates, faster convergence, and substantially lower human intervention effort than prior approaches. Moreover, the learned policies exhibit more stable and human-aligned behavior throughout training.

</details>


### [152] [Constrained MPC-Based Motion Planning for Morphing Quadrotors in Ultra-Narrow Passages under Limited Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15999)
*Harsh Modi,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于变形四旋翼飞行器在极端受限环境下的运动规划框架，设计了平滑指数障碍成本函数替代传统人工势场法，实现基于2D激光雷达的窄缝穿越与避障控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工势场法在窄通道中会产生高成本值，人为阻塞可通行路径，导致飞行器无法穿越狭窄缝隙。需要一种能在窄间隙中保持低通行成本同时具备强避障能力的成本函数。

Method: 提出平滑指数障碍成本函数，引入成本缩减因子降低窄通道内的成本值，避免硬激活阈值；将2D LiDAR测量值直接用于非线性MPC框架（基于acados实现），联合规划变形四旋翼的形态与轨迹。

Result: 仿真与实验结果表明该方法能成功穿越窄走廊，而传统斥力成本函数会失败。算法计算高效，能保障安全性。

Conclusion: 该方法为变形四旋翼在紧窄空间中的导航提供了实用高效的解决方案，且成本函数设计具有通用性，可适用于任意移动机器人平台。

Abstract: This paper introduces a motion planning framework to plan morphology and trajectory for morphing quadrotors under extremely constrained environments. We develop a novel obstacle avoidance cost function for nonlinear model predictive control (MPC) that enables navigation through extremely narrow gaps under limited perception from a 2D LiDAR. Classical artificial potential field-based costs typically have a high cost in narrow passages, artificially blocking the navigable path. In contrast, we propose a smooth exponential obstacle cost that preserves low traversal cost within narrow gaps while maintaining strong collision avoidance behavior. The formulation avoids hard activation thresholds and introduces a cost reduction factor to reduce the cost within narrow passages. Direct use of 2D LiDAR measurements in MPC allows navigation around arbitrarily shaped obstacles. The method is embedded within an acados-based nonlinear MPC framework. Simulation and experimental results demonstrate successful traversal of narrow corridors where typical repulsive cost functions would fail. The approach provides a computationally efficient and practical solution for navigating through tight spaces while maintaining safety from the obstacles. While we are implementing the framework on the morphing quadrotors, the cost function formulation is general-purpose for any mobile robot application, and is not limited to the morphing quadrotors. The implementation code is available at \href{https://github.com/harshjmodi1996/morphocopter_mpc}{Github Repo} and a short video is available at \href{https://zh.engr.tamu.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/310/2026/03/MPC_MorphoCopter_video.mp4}{Video Link}.

</details>


### [153] [Fast Expanding Safe Circular Regions for Efficient Local Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16009)
*Scott Fredriksson,Akshit Saradagi,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于几何算法的局部导航方法，通过从LiDAR扫描中计算一系列朝向目标方向扩展的圆形区域来捕捉自由可导航空间，相比传统优化/学习方法具有更快计算速度和更长规划视野，并在ROS2中实现验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有局部导航方法（如动态窗口法、模型预测控制、控制屏障函数和机器学习方法）在简单环境中表现良好，但在复杂场景中面临优化或学习过程计算开销大、规划视野有限的问题，亟需一种更高效、可处理复杂环境的几何方法。

Method: 提出一种几何算法，基于局部LiDAR扫描数据计算一系列圆形区域（circular regions），这些区域沿目标方向逐步扩展，从而表征局部自由可导航空间，实现快速计算和长视野规划。

Result: 该方法在ROS2框架中实现并通过模拟环境评估，相比传统方法具有更快的计算时间和更长的规划视野。

Conclusion: 几何算法路径规划方法在复杂环境中能克服优化/学习方法的局限性，提供高效、长视野的局部导航能力，适用于实际机器人系统。

Abstract: Local navigation is one of the fundamental problems in robot navigation, and numerous approaches have been proposed over the years, including methods such as the Dynamic Window Approach, Model Predictive Control, and more recently, Control Barrier Functions and machine learning based techniques. While these methods perform well in simple environments, many of them rely on optimization or learning based procedures that can struggle in more complex scenarios. In contrast, this article proposes a more geometric algorithmic approach that enables a local navigation method with faster computation times and longer planning horizons. The proposed method is based on the computation of a sequence of circular regions from a local LiDAR scan that expand in the direction of the goal and capture free local navigable space. The proposed method was implemented in the ROS2 framework and evaluated in a simulated environment.

</details>


### [154] [Adaptive Outer-Loop Control of Quadrotors via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16015)
*Vishnu Saj,Sushi Vemuri,Dileep Kalathil,Moble Benedict*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合残差动力学预测器(RDP)、线性校准桥和在线推力修正机制的自适应控制架构，使四旋翼无人机在动态干扰下实现精确轨迹跟踪，并在Crazyflie微型四旋翼上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于域随机化的深度强化学习方法在sim-to-real迁移中会产生过于保守的策略，难以应对动态干扰，需要一种能主动感知并实时响应扰动的自适应控制架构。

Method: 1) 训练最优外环策略；2) 用残差动力学预测器(RDP)替代对真实扰动数据的依赖，通过状态和控制历史在线估计外力和力矩；3) 引入数据高效的线性校准桥和在线推力修正机制，仅需数秒飞行数据即可对齐仿真与真实潜空间。

Result: 在Crazyflie微型四旋翼上的真实世界验证表明，所提出的自适应控制器在质量变化、非对称载荷和动态悬挂负载等严重不确定条件下，显著优于基线方法，保持精确的轨迹跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的自适应控制架构有效解决了DRL在四旋翼飞行控制中因域随机化导致的策略保守性问题，通过在线扰动感知与补偿实现了鲁棒的sim-to-real迁移。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) for quadrotor flight control typically relies on Domain Randomization (DR) for sim-to-real transfer, resulting in overly conservative policies that struggle with dynamic disturbances. To overcome this, we propose a novel adaptive control architecture that actively perceives and reacts to instantaneous perturbations. First, we train an optimal outer-loop policy, then replace its reliance on ground-truth disturbance data with a Residual Dynamics Predictor (RDP). The RDP estimates the external forces and moments acting on the aircraft in flight online using only the history of states and control actions. For seamless hardware transfer, we introduce a data-efficient linear calibration bridge and an online thrust correction mechanism that align the simulated latent space with reality using mere seconds of flight data. Real-world validations on a Crazyflie micro-quadrotor demonstrate that our adaptive controller significantly outperforms baselines, maintaining precise trajectory tracking under severe uncertainties including mass variations, asymmetric payloads, and dynamic slung loads

</details>


### [155] [Learning Sim-Grounded Policies for Bimanual Rope Manipulation from Human Teleoperation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16043)
*Gina Wigginghaus,Tim Missal,Berk Guler,Simon Manschitz,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在绳索解结任务中，基于状态（DLO 3D粒子状态）的模仿学习策略相比基于视觉（腕部相机RGB流）的策略具有更好的泛化能力，将L1误差降低了30.8%，揭示了像素空间与物理一致状态空间之间的可观测性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形线性物体（DLO）的操控因高维构型空间和自遮挡而极具挑战。遥操作模仿学习虽可行但受限于人力成本，因此观测空间的选择对从小数据集中实现泛化至关重要。本文旨在探究视觉策略泛化能力不足的根本原因是否在于观测空间本身而非策略架构或数据规模。

Method: 在相同双臂遥操作数据上训练两个Action Chunking with Transformers策略：视觉策略以腕部相机双路RGB流为输入，状态策略以DLO的3D粒子状态为输入（通过多视角融合从初始观测提取，并在基于粒子的eXtended Position-Based Dynamics仿真中演化）。在未见过的绳索构型上进行开环评估。

Result: 状态策略在预测初始抓取拉动动作时的L1误差比视觉策略低30.8%，量化了像素与物理一致状态之间的可观测性差距。

Conclusion: 基于物理一致状态表示的策略能显著提升小样本模仿学习在DLO操控任务中的泛化能力，为从有限人工演示中实现更数据高效的机器人学习指明了方向。

Abstract: Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs) such as ropes and cables are widely encountered in both household and industrial applications, yet remain challenging to manipulate due to their infinite-dimensional configuration space and frequent self-occlusion. Imitation learning from teleoperation offers a practical path to bimanual DLO manipulation, but its scalability is limited by human effort, making the choice of observation space critical for generalization from small datasets. In this study, we investigate whether the lack of generalization in egocentric visual policies for the knot-untangling task stems from the observation space itself, rather than from the policy architecture or data scale. We compare two Action Chunking with Transformers policies trained on the same bimanual teleoperation data: a vision-based policy conditioned on two egocentric RGB streams from wrist-mounted cameras, and a state-based policy conditioned on the DLO's 3D particle state, extracted from an initial observation via multi-view fusion and evolved in a particle-based eXtended Position-Based Dynamics simulation. Evaluated open-loop on an unseen rope configuration, the state-based policy outperforms its visual counterpart with a 30.8% reduction in L1 error when predicting the initial grasp-and-pull action, quantifying the observability gap between pixels and physics-consistent state, and pointing toward more data-efficient robot learning for the DLO manipulation task from limited human demonstrations.

</details>


### [156] [Health-Conditioned Vision-Language-Action Models for Malfunction-Aware Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16056)
*Hüseyin Arslan,Özgür Erkent*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种健康感知的VLA模型，通过引入健康投影模块，使机器人能够在关节退化等物理故障情况下仍能完成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽然能检测和恢复任务失败，但缺乏对机器人物理退化（如关节退化、执行器故障、夹爪无力）的适应能力，无法在真实场景中应对机器人物理故障。

Method: 在VLA-Adapter架构中注入健康投影模块，以机器人的关节角度和扭矩能力作为健康向量输入，并在LIBERO环境上收集的故障机器人数据上进行训练。

Result: 在Libero-Spatial任务上，该方法仅添加轻量级模块就能在不同退化关节配置下成功完成任务，而预训练的VLA-Adapter模型无法做到。

Conclusion: 健康感知（即考虑机器人物理状态）对于VLA模型在实际场景中应对物理退化至关重要，轻量级的健康条件化方法即可有效提升模型的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Research on Vision Language Action (VLA) models has been increasing rapidly in recent years. Although some of them focus on detecting, preventing, and recovering from task failures, they usually don't deal with adapting to robot's physical failures. In real-life scenarios, most robots face physical degradations in various ways such as joint degradation, actuator failure, or weak gripper. We introduce malfunction-aware (health-conditioned) VLA that takes a health vector as an input that gives information about robots' joints' operation angle and torque capability, and adapts its predictions to complete the tasks with the degraded joints. To achieve this, we inject a Health Projector module to the VLA-Adapter architecture and train it on malfunction robot data we collected on the LIBERO environment [1]. We collect 128 teleoperated episodes on Libero-Spatial tasks. Our results show that, with a very lightweight addition, the model can learn to operate successfully with different configurations of degraded joints which the default pretrained VLA-Adapter's Libero-Spatial-Pro model cannot. The code and dataset will be available soon at https://github.com/h-arslan/health-aware-vla

</details>


### [157] [Beyond Collision Avoidance: Multi-Robot Yielding and Spatial Affordance in Emergency Evacuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16115)
*Ning Zhou,Edmund R. Hunt,Nikolai W. F. Bode*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过游戏化虚拟疏散实验（N=56），研究了四种多机器人避让策略在受限走廊环境中的心理效应，建立了偏好层级：Hide > LineEscape > Freeze > ShortestPath。研究发现环境可供性显著影响认知期望，主动利用避难凹槽能增强心理舒适度，而忽视明显凹槽会引发期望违背和认知延迟感知。实验表明，紧急疏散中的人机交互需从纯轨迹优化转向语义感知导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同避让策略多聚焦于碰撞避免和宏观流量优化，忽视了环境可供性和人类空间期望。为弥合宏观理论与微观感知之间的差距，研究者开展了此项工作。

Method: 采用游戏化虚拟疏散实验方法，招募56名被试，在有无避难凹槽的受限走廊中测试四种多机器人避让策略（Hide、LineEscape、Freeze、ShortestPath），通过主观问卷测量心理反应。

Result: 1) 建立了稳健的偏好层级：Hide > LineEscape > Freeze > ShortestPath；2) 主动空间避让（Hide）显著优于冻结和效率优先策略；3) 环境可供性塑造认知期望，利用避难凹槽增强舒适度；4) 忽视明显凹槽（LineEscape）会引发期望违背，显著增加感知认知延迟；5) 先前机器人交互经验有助于理解复杂社交意图。

Conclusion: 紧急疏散中的人机交互安全必须从纯轨迹优化转向语义感知导航。未来工作将扩展至机器人群体与行人人群的复杂交互研究。

Abstract: As mobile service robots increasingly coexist with pedestrians, ensuring passively safe behaviour during confined emergency evacuations is critical. Existing multi-robot yielding strategies often focus solely on collision avoidance and macroscopic flow optimisation, overlooking environmental affordances and human spatial expectations. To bridge the gap between macroscopic theory and micro-level perception, we conducted a game-based virtual evacuation experiment (N=56). We investigated individual psychological responses to four multi-robot yielding strategies (Hide, LineEscape, Freeze, ShortestPath) across confined corridors with and without refuge niches. Our results establish a robust preference hierarchy (Hide > LineEscape > Freeze > ShortestPath), demonstrating that proactive space-yielding significantly outperforms freezing and efficiency-first approaches. Crucially, we found that environmental affordances heavily shape cognitive expectations. Actively utilising available niches amplifies the psychological comfort of proactive yielding (Hide). Conversely, failing to use an obvious niche (e.g., executing LineEscape) may trigger Expectation Violation. This is reflected in a drastically increased perceived cognitive delay, despite objectively unimpeded trajectories. Furthermore, prior robot interaction experience helps users decode complex social intents. Ultimately, this research demonstrates that safe human-robot interaction during emergencies must evolve from pure trajectory optimisation to semantically aware navigation. Future work will extend this framework to investigate complex interactions between robot swarms and pedestrian crowds.

</details>


### [158] [DexJoCo: A Benchmark and Toolkit for Task-Oriented Dexterous Manipulation on MuJoCo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16257)
*Hanwen Wang,Weizhi Zhao,Xiangyu Wang,Siyuan Huang,He Lin,Boyuan Zheng,Rongtao Xu,Gang Wang,Yao Mu,He Wang,Lue Fan,Hongsheng Li,Zhaoxiang Zhang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DexJoCo，一个面向灵巧手任务导向操控的基准测试与工具包，包含11个功能基础任务、1.1K轨迹数据，并系统评估了现代策略在视觉/动力学随机化、多任务训练等设置下的表现，揭示了当前灵巧操控策略的共性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的灵巧操控基准缺乏能反映灵巧手相对于平行夹爪独特操控能力（如工具使用、双手协调、长时程执行、推理）的任务，也缺少全面的评估管线。

Method: 构建了11个功能基础的任务集；开发低成本数据采集系统，收集1.1K轨迹；支持域随机化以评估鲁棒性；在视觉/动力学随机化、多任务训练、动作头适配等多种设置下对现代策略进行基准测试。

Result: 通过大量实证分析，识别出当前灵巧操控策略中的重要洞察和常见局限性。

Conclusion: DexJoCo为灵巧手机器人学习提供了标准化评估平台，揭示了当前方法的不足，并指出了未来研究的关键挑战。

Abstract: Achieving human-level manipulation requires dexterous robotic hands capable of complex object interactions. Advancing such capabilities further demands standardized benchmarks for systematic evaluation. However, existing dexterous benchmarks lack tasks that reflect the unique manipulation capabilities of dexterous hands over parallel grippers, as well as comprehensive evaluation pipelines. In this paper, we present DexJoCo, a benchmark and toolkit for task-oriented dexterous manipulation, comprising 11 functionally grounded tasks that evaluate tool-use, bimanual coordination, long-horizon execution, and reasoning. We develop a low-cost data collection system and collect 1.1K trajectories across these tasks, with support for domain randomization to assess robustness. We benchmark modern models under diverse settings, including visual and dynamics randomization, multi-task training, and action-head adaptation. Through extensive empirical analysis, we identify several important insights and common limitations of current policies in dexterous manipulation, highlighting key challenges for future research in dexterous hand robot learning. Project page available at: https://dexjoco.github.io

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [DeepSlide: From Artifacts to Presentation Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15202)
*Ming Yang,Zhiwei Zhang,Jiahang Li,Haoseng Liu,Yuzheng Cai,Weiguo Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepSlide 是一个人在回路的多智能体系统，专注于优化演示文稿的完整准备过程（包括叙事规划、时间预算、脚本生成、注意力增强和排练支持），而不仅仅是生成视觉上合理的幻灯片。它引入了一个双评分板基准，将静态工件质量与动态演讲表现分开评估，在20个领域和多种受众场景下，与强基线模型相比，在保持工件质量的同时，在叙事流畅性、节奏精度、幻灯片-脚本协同和注意力引导等交付指标上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI幻灯片生成器主要优化生成视觉上合理的演示文稿工件，但忽视了演讲的交付过程（节奏、叙事和演讲准备），导致生成的幻灯片虽好看却难以有效支持实际演讲。

Method: DeepSlide 包含四个核心组件：(i) 可控逻辑链规划器（含每节点时间预算），(ii) 轻量级内容树检索器用于证据锚定，(iii) 马尔可夫式顺序渲染（含样式继承），(iv) 沙盒执行与最小修复确保可渲染性。系统采用人在回路的多智能体架构，覆盖从需求获取到排练支持的全流程。

Result: 在20个领域和多种受众场景下，DeepSlide 在幻灯片工件质量上与强基线模型持平，同时在交付指标上取得显著提升，包括更好的叙事流畅性、节奏精度、幻灯片-脚本协同性以及更清晰的注意力引导。

Conclusion: 通过将优化目标从静态工件质量扩展到动态演讲交付过程，DeepSlide 在保持高质量幻灯片生成的同时显著提升了演讲的整体效果，证明了综合考虑内容和交付过程的AI辅助演讲准备方法的有效性。

Abstract: Presentations are a primary medium for scholarly communication, yet most AI slide generators optimize the artifact (a visually plausible deck) while under-optimizing the delivery process (pacing, narrative, and presentation preparation). We present DeepSlide, a human-in-the-loop multi-agent system that supports preparing the full presentation process, from requirement elicitation and time-budgeted narrative planning, to evidence-grounded slide--script generation, attention augmentation, and rehearsal support. DeepSlide integrates (i) a controllable logical-chain planner with per-node time budgets, (ii) a lightweight content-tree retriever for grounding, (iii) Markov-style sequential rendering with style inheritance, and (iv) sandboxed execution with minimal repair to ensure renderability. We further introduce a dual-scoreboard benchmark that cleanly separates static artifact quality from dynamic delivery excellence. Across 20 domains and diverse audience profiles, DeepSlide matches strong baselines on artifact quality while consistently achieving larger gains on delivery metrics, improving narrative flow, pacing precision, and slide--script synergy with clearer attention guidance.

</details>


### [160] [Does Theory of Mind Improvement Really Benefit Human-AI Interactions? Empirical Findings from Interactive Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15205)
*Nanxu Gong,Zixin Chen,Haotian Li,Zishu Zhao,Jianxun Lian,Huamin Qu,Yanjie Fu,Xing Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有的大语言模型心智理论（ToM）评估基准大多基于静态的第三人称故事阅读理解，忽略了人-AI交互中第一人称、动态和开放式的特点。本文提出了交互式ToM评估新范式，系统研究了四种ToM增强技术，发现静态基准上的提升并不总能转化为动态交互中的更好表现，强调了基于交互评估的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ToM基准（故事阅读、第三人称多项选择）无法反映人-AI交互中第一人称、动态、开放式的真实场景，需要提出能直接衡量ToM提升技术如何惠及人-AI交互的新评估范式。

Method: 提出交互式ToM评估新范式，包含视角转换（第三人称→第一人称）和度量转换（静态选择→动态交互表现）；系统研究四种代表性ToM增强技术，使用四个真实数据集和一个用户研究，覆盖目标导向任务（编程、数学）和体验导向任务（咨询）。

Result: 静态基准上的ToM改进并不总能转化为动态HAI交互中更好的性能表现。

Conclusion: 基于交互的评估对于开发下一代具有社会感知能力的LLM至关重要，为ToM评估提供了关键见解。

Abstract: Improving the Theory of Mind (ToM) capability of Large Language Models (LLMs) is crucial for effective social interactions between these AI models and humans. However, the existing benchmarks often measure ToM capability improvement through story-reading, multiple-choice questions from a third-person perspective, while ignoring the first-person, dynamic, and open-ended nature of human-AI (HAI) interactions. To directly examine how ToM improvement techniques benefit HAI interactions, we first proposed the new paradigm of interactive ToM evaluation with both perspective and metric shifts. Next, following the paradigm, we conducted a systematic study of four representative ToM enhancement techniques using both four real-world datasets and a user study, covering both goal-oriented tasks (e.g., coding, math) and experience-oriented tasks (e.g., counseling). Our findings reveal that improvements on static benchmarks do not always translate to better performance in dynamic HAI interactions. This paper offers critical insights into ToM evaluation, showing the necessity of interaction-based assessments in developing next-generation, socially aware LLMs for HAI symbiosis.

</details>


### [161] [NOVA: Fundamental Limits of Knowledge Discovery Through AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15219)
*Salman Avestimehr,Ken Duffy,Muriel Médard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NOVA框架，将AI系统的自改进循环建模为知识空间上的自适应采样过程。识别了四种失败模式，揭示了“污染陷阱”机制（当易发现知识耗尽时，假阳性导致无效知识污染），并证明在Zipf律假设下，累计发现成本随发现量呈Θ(c_gen D^α)的幂律增长，最后形式化了人类增强的三种方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI系统能否通过迭代自改进真正发现新知识，以及需要付出的成本代价，并揭示该过程中的失败机制。

Method: 提出NOVA框架，将“生成-验证-积累-重训练”循环建模为知识空间上的自适应采样过程；利用Good-Turing估计进行局部批次多样性诊断；在Zipf尾等价假设下推导成本标度律。

Result: 识别了四种失败模式：污染、遗忘、探索失败、接受失败；揭示了“污染陷阱”机制；证明了累计发现成本的幂律标度律R_cum(D)=Θ(c_gen D^α)；形式化了人类增强的三种方式（指导、生成、验证）。

Conclusion: AI系统在理想条件下可通过迭代自改进覆盖有限知识空间，但受污染陷阱和成本幂律增长的制约；专家输入在自主探索瓶颈处最有效。

Abstract: Can AI systems discover genuinely new knowledge through iterative self improvement, and if so, at what cost? We introduce the NOVA framework, which models the common ``generate, verify, accumulate, retrain'' loop as an adaptive sampling process over a knowledge space. We identify sufficient conditions under which accumulated genuine knowledge eventually covers a finite domain, and show how their violations produce distinct failure modes: contamination, forgetting, exploration failure, and acceptance failure. We then analyze imperfect verification and identify a contamination trap: as easy-to-find knowledge is exhausted, the model mass assigned to new valid artifacts shrinks, so even small false-positive rates can cause invalid artifacts to enter the knowledge base faster than genuine discoveries. We clarify that Good--Turing estimation is a local batch-diversity diagnostic, not an estimator of the historically undiscovered valid mass that governs long-term discovery. Under a separate tail-equivalence assumption relating the model's effective discovery distribution to a Zipf law with exponent $α>1$, we prove that the cumulative generation cost required to obtain $D$ distinct genuine discoveries satisfies $R_{\mathrm{cum}}(D)=Θ(c_{\mathrm{gen}}D^α)$, where $c_{\mathrm{gen}}$ is the per-candidate generation cost. This scaling law quantifies asymptotic diminishing returns as the discovery frontier advances. Finally, we formalize human amplification through guidance, generation, and verification, explaining why expert input is most valuable near autonomous exploration barriers.

</details>


### [162] [NIMO Controller: a self-driving laboratory orchestrator based on the Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15227)
*Naruki Yoshikawa,Ryo Tamura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型上下文协议（MCP）的自驱动实验室软件架构，通过NIMO Controller实现可视化编程界面和统一接口，同时支持人类用户和AI智能体操作SDL，并在色彩匹配案例中验证了其可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自驱动实验室（SDL）的编排软件框架主要面向人类交互设计，缺乏适合AI智能体的标准化接口，开发SDL软件技术门槛高，限制了科学发现的自动化进程。

Method: 提出基于MCP的SDL软件架构，将所有SDL功能通过MCP服务器暴露；开发NIMO Controller编排器，通过MCP工具发现自动生成可视化编程界面；同一MCP后端同时支持人类用户和AI智能体访问。

Result: 在色彩匹配SDL案例研究中验证了所提MCP架构的可用性，证明其能同时服务人类用户和AI智能体。

Conclusion: 基于MCP的SDL架构提供了一种统一、标准化的接口方案，降低了SDL软件开发门槛，并为人类与AI协同操作SDL提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories (SDLs) have attracted increasing attention as a means of accelerating scientific discovery; however, developing SDL software remains technically demanding. To improve accessibility, orchestration software frameworks have been proposed to coordinate SDL components. Nevertheless, existing frameworks are primarily designed for human interaction and do not provide standardized interfaces suitable for AI agents. In this work, we propose an SDL software architecture based on the Model Context Protocol (MCP), in which all SDL functionalities are exposed through MCP servers. Following this design principle, we introduce an MCP-based SDL orchestrator, named NIMO Controller. It provides a visual programming interface automatically generated through MCP-based tool discovery, allowing human users to design experimental workflows without writing code. The same MCP backend can also be accessed by AI agents, providing a unified interface for both human users and AI agents. We demonstrate the proposed system through a case study on a color-matching SDL. The results validate the usability of the proposed MCP-based SDL architecture.

</details>


### [163] [Zero-Shot Goal Recognition with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15333)
*Kin Max Piamolini Gusmão,Nathan Gavenski,Nir Oren,Felipe Meneguzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了前沿LLM在经典PDDL规划领域中进行零样本目标识别的能力，发现模型在证据整合上存在根本性差异：部分模型能随观测证据扩展并接近地标级准确率，而另一些模型始终固守世界知识先验，无法有效利用新证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标识别（从观测序列推断智能体目标）是一种溯因任务，天然适合LLM利用世界知识进行一致性评估，而非生成动作序列。这比直接规划更适合LLM，且能更好地评估其规划基础能力。

Method: 在经典PDDL规划基准上，对前沿LLM进行零样本目标识别评估，并与地标方法对比；通过定性分析模型推理痕迹，区分证据整合能力与领域熟悉度的影响。

Result: LLM在目标识别上的能力不均衡：部分模型随观测证据增加而提升准确率，接近地标方法；另一些模型无论证据多少，始终依赖世界知识先验。

Conclusion: 目标识别可作为评估LLM规划基础知识的原理性基准，并揭示了LLM在证据整合能力上的根本差异，而非简单的领域知识差异。

Abstract: Large language models have recently reached near-parity with classical planners on well-known planning domains, yet this competence relies on world-knowledge exploitation rather than genuine symbolic reasoning. Goal recognition is a complementary abductive task structurally better suited to LLM strengths: it consists of evaluating consistency with world knowledge rather than generating novel action sequences. This paper provides the first systematic zero-shot evaluation of frontier LLMs as goal recognisers on key classical PDDL benchmarks. Our results show that LLM competence on goal recognition is uneven: some models scale with evidence and approach landmark-based accuracy at full observations, while others remain anchored to world-knowledge priors regardless of how much evidence accumulates. Qualitative analysis of model reasoning traces reveals that this divergence reflects a fundamental difference in evidence integration rather than domain familiarity. These findings position goal recognition as a principled benchmark for the foundational planning knowledge of LLMs.

</details>


### [164] [Belief Engine: Configurable and Inspectable Stance Dynamics in Multi-Agent LLM Deliberation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15343)
*Joshua C. Yang,Maurice Flechtner,Damian Dailisan,Michiel A. Bakker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Belief Engine (BE)，一个可审计的信念更新层，将智能体的信念建模为关于命题的证据状态，通过参数 u（证据吸收）和 a（先验锚定）控制立场变化，并在 DEBATE 数据集上验证其能够重构遵循证据的人类立场变化轨迹，同时揭示锚定和证据之外因素对稳定立场的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的智能体在模拟协商、冲突解决等互动时，产生的对话记录无法揭示立场变化的原因（如证据吸收、锚定、角色漂移、回音效应等），缺乏可审计的信念更新机制。

Method: 将信念定义为关于命题的证据状态并暴露为标量立场；将论点提取到结构化记忆中，使用对数几率更新规则，由证据吸收系数 u 和先验锚定系数 a 控制立场更新。

Result: 在多个基础 LLM 上，参数扫描表明 u 和 a 能可靠地塑造立场动态，同时保留证据级别的更新轨迹。在 DEBATE 数据集上，BE 能最佳重构那些最终立场遵循提取证据的人类参与者；立场稳定或与证据相反的案例则归因于锚定或证据流之外的因素。

Conclusion: BE 提供了可配置的基础设施，用于研究基于证据的 deliberation，将开放性、承诺、收敛和分歧等行为与显式的更新假设而非隐式提示效应联系起来。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly used to simulate deliberative interactions such as negotiation, conflict resolution, and multi-turn opinion exchange. Yet generated transcripts often do not reveal why an agent's stance changes: movement may reflect evidence uptake, anchoring, role drift, echoing, or changed prompt and retrieval context. We introduce the Belief Engine (BE), an auditable belief-update layer that treats "belief" as an evidential state over a proposition and exposes it as scalar stance. BE extracts arguments into structured memory and updates stance with a log-odds rule controlled by evidence uptake u and prior anchoring a. Across multiple base LLMs, parameter sweeps show that these controls reliably shape stance dynamics while preserving an evidence-level update trail. On DEBATE, a human deliberation dataset with pre/post opinions, BE best reconstructs participants whose final stance follows extracted evidence; stable and evidence-opposed cases instead point to anchoring or factors outside the extracted evidence stream. BE provides configurable infrastructure for studying evidence-grounded deliberation, where openness, commitment, convergence, and disagreement can be tied to explicit update assumptions rather than hidden prompt effects.

</details>


### [165] [Ensemble Monitoring for AI Control: Diverse Signals Outweigh More Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15377)
*Eugene Koran,Yejun Yun,Samantha Tetef,Benjamin Arnav,Pablo Bernabeu-Pérez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过集成多样化监控器（基于GPT-4.1-Mini，结合提示工程和微调策略）来检测AI智能体的不安全行为。实验表明，多样性驱动的集成监控比同质集成或单一监控器在检测对抗性编码任务中的错位行为上效果更优（最佳3监控器集成性能提升2.4倍），且微调模型在分布外攻击类型上仍保持优势，支持集成监控作为实用的AI控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统在大规模自主智能体场景中的部署日益增多，确保其行为安全并与用户意图对齐变得至关重要。监控智能体行为是关键安全机制，但可靠监控器难以构建，且系统规模使得人类监督不切实际。

Method: 基于GPT-4.1-Mini构建12个监控器，采用提示工程和微调两种策略；在编码任务中，对通过标准测试但无法通过对抗性输入的候选方案进行检测；通过集成多样化监控器（结合强个体性能与低相关性）来提升检测效果。

Result: 多样化集成监控器优于个体监控器和同质集成监控器；最佳3监控器集成相较3个相同监控器组成的集成，检测性能提升2.4倍；该集成在独立数据集上同样表现强劲；微调监控器在所有最优集成中均出现，并在分布外攻击类型上保持优势。

Conclusion: 多样性（而非规模）是集成监控性能提升的关键驱动因素；微调能够激发提示工程无法获得的检测能力；集成监控是一种实用的AI控制策略，能在合理推理成本下实现安全增益。

Abstract: As AI systems are increasingly deployed in autonomous agentic settings at scale, it is important to ensure the actions they take are safe and aligned with user intent. Monitoring agent actions is a key safety mechanism, yet reliable monitors remain difficult to build and the scale of these systems makes human oversight impractical. We show that combining signals from diverse monitors into an ensemble improves detection of misaligned actions. We build 12 GPT-4.1-Mini monitors using both prompting and fine-tuning strategies. We evaluate them on coding tasks where candidate solutions pass standard tests but fail on adversarial inputs. In this setting, diverse ensembles outperform both individual monitors and homogeneous ensembles. Our best 3-monitor ensemble achieves 2.4x greater detection performance gain compared to an ensemble composed of three identical monitors, with the same ensemble performing strongly on an independent dataset. We contend that these results show that diversity - not scale - drives gains. The best ensembles combine strong individual performance with low correlation between monitors. Furthermore, fine-tuned monitors appear in every top-performing ensemble and maintain this advantage on out-of-distribution attack types, suggesting that fine-tuning enables detection capabilities that prompting alone does not elicit. These results support ensemble monitoring as a practical AI control strategy for safety gains at reasonable inference costs.

</details>


### [166] [From LLM-Generated Conjectures to Lean Formalizations: Automated Polynomial Inequality Proving via Sum-of-Squares Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15445)
*Ruobing Zuo,Hanrui Zhao,Gaolei He,Zhengfeng Yang,Jianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NSPI架构，结合LLM启发式搜索与符号计算验证，通过近似多项式平方和分解再精确化，实现可扩展的多变量多项式不等式自动证明，并在Lean中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项式不等式自动证明面临纯符号方法在高维/高次时扩展性差、中间表达式爆炸的问题，以及现有LLM方法多限于少变量竞赛级不等式的局限。

Method: LLM提出近似多项式平方和(SOS)分解猜想，经符号计算精炼为精确多项式SOS表示，进而证明目标不等式，最后在Lean定理证明器中完成机器验证。

Result: 在包含多达10个变量的多项式不等式基准测试上验证了方法的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法NSPI有效结合了LLM的启发式探索能力与符号计算的严格保证，实现了从启发式发现到机器验证的端到端流水线，突破了多项式不等式证明的规模瓶颈。

Abstract: Automated proving of polynomial inequalities is a fundamental challenge in automated mathematical reasoning, where rich algebraic structure and a rapidly growing certificate search space hinder scalability. Purely symbolic approaches provide strong guarantees but often scale poorly as the number of variables or the degree increases, due to expensive algebraic manipulations and rapidly growing intermediate expressions. In parallel, LLM-guided methods have made notable progress, particularly on competition-style inequalities with a small number of variables. To address the remaining scalability challenges, we propose NSPI, a neuro-symbolic framework that combines the complementary strengths of LLMs and symbolic computation for polynomial-inequality proving. Concretely, an LLM proposes a conjecture in the form of an approximate polynomial Sum-Of-Squares (SOS) decomposition; we refine it via symbolic computation to obtain an exact polynomial SOS representation, which directly proves the target inequality, and we further certify the proof in Lean, yielding an end-to-end pipeline from heuristic discovery to machine-checked proof. Experiments on challenging benchmarks involving polynomials with up to 10 variables demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [167] [CAPS: Cascaded Adaptive Pairwise Selection for Efficient Parallel Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15513)
*Fangzhou Lin,Shuo Xing,Peiran Li,Siyuan Yang,Qianwen Ge,Kazunori Yamada,Ziming Zhang,Haichong Zhang,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAPS提出了一种级联自适应配对选择框架，通过沿证据轴和分布轴非均匀分配验证器计算资源，在代码和数学推理基准上以更少的验证token实现了优于现有配对验证方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对自验证是LLM测试时扩展中最有效的聚合原语之一，但现有方法对每个问题执行数十次完整解决方案的比较判断，无论比较是否有信息量，导致计算成本高昂。

Method: CAPS是一个仅推理阶段的框架，沿两个正交轴非均匀分配验证器计算：(1) 证据轴：自适应调整每个候选方案被判断时可见的内容量；(2) 分布轴：自适应调整比较在候选池中的分布方式。具体实现为一个四阶段级联流程，附带可选的救援子程序，并具有封闭形式的验证器token成本公式，使每个候选的边际成本相比均匀全证据调度减少约一半。

Result: 在四个自验证模型和五个推理基准上，CAPS在20个测试套件中的14个上优于领先的成对验证器，同时仅使用其25.4%的验证器token预算；在所有20个测试套件上优于逐点自验证。可对部分证据与全证据下的验证器准确率进行可解释诊断，为级联适用性提供部署前检查依据。

Conclusion: CAPS通过自适应计算分配有效降低了成对验证的计算成本，同时保持甚至提升性能，为LLM测试时扩展中的验证计算优化提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Parallel reasoning, where a generator samples many candidate solutions and an aggregator selects the best, is one of the most effective forms of test-time scaling in large language models, and pairwise self-verification has become its strongest aggregation primitive. Yet pairwise verification carries a heavy cost: each judgment reads two complete solutions in full, and existing methods perform tens of such judgments per problem regardless of whether the comparison is informative. We introduce CAPS (Cascaded Adaptive Pairwise Selection), an inference-only framework that allocates verifier compute non-uniformly along two orthogonal axes: an evidence axis that adapts how much of each candidate the judge sees, and a distribution axis that adapts how comparisons are spread across the pool. CAPS instantiates these into a four-stage cascade with an optional rescue subroutine, and admits a closed-form verifier-token cost in which the per-candidate marginal cost is roughly halved relative to uniform full-evidence schedules. On four self-verifying models (Qwen3-14B, GPT-OSS-20B, Qwen3-4B-Instruct/Thinking) and five reasoning benchmarks spanning code (LiveCodeBench-v5/v6, CodeContests) and math (AIME 2025, HMMT 2025), CAPS outperforms the leading pairwise verifier on 14 of 20 suites while using 25.4% of its verifier-token budget on code, and outperforms pointwise self-verification on all 20. The trade-off suites admit an interpretable diagnostic in terms of the verifier's accuracy at partial versus full evidence, providing a concrete pre-deployment check for cascade suitability.

</details>


### [168] [RTL-BenchMT: Dynamic Maintenance of RTL Generation Benchmark Through Agent-Assisted Analysis and Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15537)
*Jing Wang,Shang Liu,Hangan Zhou,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RTL-BenchMT，一个用于动态维护RTL生成基准的自动化智能体框架，能够自动识别和修正基准中的缺陷案例以及检测和更新过拟合案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RTL基准面临两个关键挑战：(1)基准中存在缺陷案例；(2)模型对基准过拟合。这些问题纯靠人工维护难以解决，亟需自动化方法降低人力成本。

Method: 提出RTL-BenchMT智能体框架，包含两个核心应用：(1)自动识别和修正有缺陷的基准案例；(2)自动检测和更新过拟合案例。

Result: 利用RTL-BenchMT对缺陷案例和过拟合案例进行了深入分析，产出经精炼的基准套件，并将开源给社区。

Conclusion: RTL-BenchMT通过自动化智能体框架有效解决了RTL基准维护中的两大难题，降低了人工维护成本，提升了基准质量。

Abstract: This paper introduces RTL-BenchMT, an agentic framework for dynamically maintaining RTL generation benchmarks. Large Language Models (LLMs) assisted automated RTL generation is one of the most important directions in EDA research. However, current RTL benchmarks face two critical challenges: (1) flawed cases in the benchmarks and (2) overfitting to the benchmarks. Both challenges are difficult to resolve purely by manual engineering effort. To address these issues and systematically reduce human maintenance costs, we propose an automated agentic framework, RTL-BenchMT. RTL-BenchMT focuses on two key applications: (1) automatically identifying and revising flawed benchmark cases and (2) automatically detecting and updating overfitting cases. With the assistance of RTL-BenchMT, we conduct a thorough, in-depth analysis of flawed and overfitting cases and produce a refined benchmark suite that will be open-sourced to the community.

</details>


### [169] [Position: Artificial Intelligence Needs Meta Intelligence -- the Case for Metacognitive AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15567)
*Sergei Chuprov,Richard D. Lange,Leon Reznik,Paulo Shakarian,Raman Zatsarenko,Dmitrii Korobeinikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张将元认知作为通用设计原则，通过让AI系统监控自身状态并根据问题难度或错误成本动态分配资源，以提升其准确性、安全性和效率。论文从资源理性AI和心理学/认知科学的元认知策略中汲取灵感，识别了将元认知嵌入AI设计的具体挑战，并通过联邦学习案例展示了上述原则在提升学习效率、效果和安全性方面的实践效果，同时提出了一个支持元认知AI应用的软件框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统缺乏对自身认知状态的感知和资源分配的灵活性，导致在处理不同难度问题时效率低下、安全性不足。作者认为将元认知作为设计原则，可以让AI更智能地管理自身计算资源，从而提升整体性能。

Method: 论文采用论述与案例相结合的方式。首先从资源理性AI和心理学/认知科学的元认知策略中梳理理论基础，识别设计挑战；然后通过联邦学习案例展示元认知原则的具体应用效果；最后提出一个支持元认知AI应用的新型软件框架。

Result: 在联邦学习案例中，元认知原则实现了学习效率、学习效果和安全性三方面的改进。此外，作者开发了一个让社区能够设计、部署和实验元认知AI应用的软件框架。

Conclusion: 元认知作为AI设计原则具有潜力，能够提升系统的准确性、安全性和效率。但该领域仍存在开放的理论和实现问题需要进一步研究。

Abstract: This position paper argues for metacognition as a general design principle for creating more accurate, secure, and efficient AI. The metacognitive solution involves systems monitoring their own states and judiciously allocating resources depending on each problem instance's difficulty or cost of mistakes. Drawing inspiration both from past work on resource-rational AI and from well-documented metacognitive strategies in psychology and cognitive science, we identify specific challenges in embedding these strategies into AI design and highlight open theoretical and implementation problems. We showcase these principles through a tangible example of improved learning efficiency, effectiveness, and security in a Federated Learning (FL) case study. We show how these principles can be translated into practice with a novel software framework developed specifically to allow the community to design, deploy, and experiment with metacognition-enabled AI applications.

</details>


### [170] [STAR: A Stage-attributed Triage and Repair framework for RCA Agents in Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15581)
*Junle Wang,Xingchuang Liao,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STAR是一个面向LLM-based RCA Agent的阶段性故障定位与修复框架，通过将RCA工作流分解为四个结构化阶段（EP、HS、AS、DR），并采用阶段审计、Fast/Slow路由、反事实候选评估和阶段特定补丁重放机制，有效提升了根因定位和故障分类的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based RCA Agent的可靠性脆弱，早期阶段的错误会沿推理链传播并最终污染诊断结果，亟需一种能够定位和修复推理链中错误的方法。

Method: 将RCA工作流显式分解为Evidence Package (EP)、Hypothesis Set (HS)、Analysis Structure (AS)和Decision Report (DR)四个阶段；基于LangGraph实现阶段审计、预算感知的Fast/Slow路由、基于反事实候选评估的 decisive 阶段定位以及阶段特定的patch-and-replay修复机制。

Result: 在公开大规模基准和真实生产数据集上，使用两种RCA Agent工作流和三种基础模型，STAR在根因定位和故障分类上持续优于强基线；能够高精度识别关键故障阶段；大多数初始错误轨迹在一到两轮重放内完成修复。

Conclusion: 显式建模RCA Agent失败发生在哪个阶段，是实现可靠、可调试、可自修复的Agentic RCA系统的有效路径。

Abstract: LLM-based root cause analysis (RCA) agents have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for incident diagnosis in microservice AIOps. However, their reliability remains fragile: an error in early evidence collection, hypothesis formulation, or causal analysis can propagate through the reasoning trace and eventually corrupt the final diagnosis. In this paper, we present \textbf{STAR}, a \emph{Stage-attributed Triage and Repair} framework for repairing erroneous RCA traces. STAR explicitly decomposes an RCA workflow into four structured stages, namely \emph{Evidence Package} (EP), \emph{Hypothesis Set} (HS), \emph{Analysis Structure} (AS), and \emph{Decision Report} (DR), and treats agent failure as a stage-localizable reasoning bug rather than a monolithic end-to-end error. Built on top of LangGraph, STAR performs stage-wise auditing, budget-aware \emph{Fast/Slow Routing}, \emph{decisive stage localization via counterfactual candidate evaluation}, and stage-specific patch-and-replay repair.
  We evaluate STAR on a public large-scale benchmark and a real-world production dataset, using two RCA agent workflows and three foundation models. Experimental results show that STAR consistently improves both root cause localization and fault type classification over strong baselines. Moreover, STAR identifies the decisive faulty stage with high accuracy, repairs most initially incorrect traces within one or two replay rounds, and benefits substantially from both Fast/Slow Routing and counterfactual stage evaluation. These results suggest that explicitly modeling \emph{where} an RCA agent fails is an effective path toward reliable, debuggable, and self-repairing agentic RCA systems.

</details>


### [171] [TopoEvo: A Topology-Aware Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Framework for Root Cause Analysis in Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15611)
*Junle Wang,Xingchuang Liao,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TopoEvo 是一个拓扑感知的自进化多智能体框架，通过度量正交多模态对齐、向量量化离散化症状令牌、假设-证据-测试工作流和自进化机制，解决微服务根因分析中的多模态噪声、级联传播和拓扑漂移挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务根因分析面临三个核心挑战：多模态可观测数据（指标、日志、追踪）存在噪声和异构性；级联故障传播放大下游症状；自动扩缩容和滚动更新导致非平稳拓扑漂移。现有LLM-based RCA智能体存在拓扑感知缺失和"症状放大偏置"问题，错误地将根因归因于症状显著的下游受害节点。

Method: TopoEvo 包含四个核心组件：(1) 度量正交多模态对齐（MOMA），将度量嵌入分解为互补子空间，通过对比学习对齐日志和追踪，降低模态冗余和稀疏性；(2) 向量量化（VQ），将拓扑增强状态离散化为可审计的症状令牌，构建症状词典；(3) 多智能体假设-证据-测试（HET）工作流，显式验证传播一致的解释，区分初始异常与放大后的下游症状；(4) 自进化机制，刷新层次化事件记忆，通过高置信度伪标签进行保守的测试时自适应，维护拓扑漂移下的鲁棒性。

Result: 通过拓扑感知的图表示学习与结构化、拓扑约束推理的结合，TopoEvo 能够有效减轻症状放大偏置，在非平稳拓扑漂移场景下保持鲁棒性，并利用可审计的症状令牌提供可解释的根因证据。

Conclusion: TopoEvo 提出了一种拓扑感知的自进化多智能体框架，通过多模态对齐、向量量化离散化、结构化推理验证和自适应漂移处理，系统性地解决了微服务根因分析中的关键挑战，为LLM-based RCA方法提供了更可靠和可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) in microservices is challenging due to (i) noisy and heterogeneous multimodal observability (metrics, logs, traces), (ii) cascading failure propagation that amplifies downstream symptoms, and (iii) non-stationary topology drift induced by autoscaling and rolling updates. Recent LLM-based RCA agents can generate tool-grounded explanations, yet they often remain topology-agnostic and suffer from \emph{symptom-amplification bias}, misattributing the root cause to salient downstream victims. We propose \textbf{TopoEvo}, a topology-aware self-evolving multi-agent framework that couples graph representation learning with structured, topology-constrained reasoning. TopoEvo first introduces \emph{Metric-orthogonal Multimodal Alignment} (MOMA), which decomposes metric embeddings into complementary subspaces and contrastively aligns logs and traces to reduce modality redundancy and sparsity, yielding stable node representations for graph encoding. It then applies \emph{Vector Quantization} (VQ) to discretize topology-enhanced states into auditable \emph{symptom tokens} with a symptom lexicon, enabling reliable retrieval and token-level evidence grounding. On top of these discrete topology cues, TopoEvo performs a multi-agent \emph{Hypothesis--Evidence--Test} (HET) workflow to explicitly verify propagation-consistent explanations and separate initiating anomalies from amplified downstream symptoms. Finally, a \emph{Self-Evolving Mechanism} refreshes hierarchical incident memory and performs conservative test-time adaptation with high-confidence pseudo-labels to maintain robustness under drift.

</details>


### [172] [ColPackAgent: Agent-Skill-Guided Hard-Particle Monte Carlo Workflows for Colloidal Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15625)
*Lijie Ding,Changwoo Do*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ColPackAgent是一个利用MCP工具服务器和智能体技能的自主运行胶体堆积蒙特卡洛模拟的智能体框架，可将模拟工具包转化为智能体辅助的研究工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型智能体缺乏专用模拟工具和工作流指令，倾向于描述而非可靠执行胶体堆积模拟工作流，因此需要将专业模拟工具包封装为智能体可调用的形式以实现自主执行。

Method: 通过MCP服务器暴露自定义colpack Python包（封装HOOMD-blue硬粒子蒙特卡洛模拟），并编码四阶段工作流合约作为智能体技能，支持交互式、端到端自动执行和按程序文件自动研究三种模式。

Result: 在3D立方体粒子、2D圆盘与胶囊二元系统、2D硬圆盘冻结转变等多个胶体堆积模拟示例中展示了系统能力，并在17个阶段特定提示下对多种LLM进行了模型性能基准比较。

Conclusion: 将领域Python包与MCP工具和可移植智能体技能相结合，是将模拟工具包转化为智能体辅助研究工作流的实用途径。

Abstract: We introduce ColPackAgent, an agent framework that autonomously runs Monte Carlo simulations of colloidal packing through a Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool server and an agent skill, whether as a standalone agent or inside an existing agent system. By harnessing the MCP server and agent skill, ColPackAgent executes a structured workflow for colloidal packing simulations, which are central to studies of phase behavior, self-assembly, and materials design. Without dedicated simulation tools and workflow instructions, general-purpose Large Language Model (LLM) agents tend to describe such workflows rather than execute them reliably. The MCP server exposes a custom-built colpack Python package that wraps HOOMD-blue hard-particle Monte Carlo, and the skill encodes a four-stage workflow contract. ColPackAgent can carry out the workflow interactively with human feedback, autonomously from an end-to-end prompt, or as autoresearch following a provided program file. We demonstrate the system in different modes with several colloidal packing simulation examples such as cube particles in 3D, a binary system of disks and capsules in 2D, and the 2D hard-disk freezing transition using autoresearch. We also compare model performance on this workflow across a panel of LLMs with 17 stage-specific prompts. This benchmark provides a stage-level check of how reliably different models follow the setup, planning, and analysis workflow. Together, these results show that pairing a domain Python package with MCP tools and a portable agent skill provides a practical route for turning a simulation toolkit into an agent-assisted research workflow.

</details>


### [173] [Nudging Beyond the Comfort Zone: Efficient Strategy-Guided Exploration for RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15726)
*Chanuk Lee,Sangwoo Park,Minki Kang,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NudgeRL提出了一种基于策略提示（Strategy Nudging）的结构化探索框架，通过在强化学习中引入策略级上下文实现多样化推理轨迹，并采用统一目标函数分解奖励信号，在数学推理任务上以更少计算量超越了GRPO和基于特权信息的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLVR方法受限于探索不足，只能从已采样的轨迹中学习；增加rollout数量虽可缓解但计算成本高昂，而现有修改优化目标的方法对探索过程的控制有限。

Method: 提出策略提示机制，在每个rollout中注入轻量级策略级上下文以生成多样化推理轨迹；设计统一目标函数，将奖励信号分解为上下文间和上下文内两部分，并引入蒸馏目标将探索到的行为迁移回基础策略。

Result: 在五个具有挑战性的数学基准测试上，NudgeRL以更少的计算量（相当于标准GRPO 8倍rollout预算的性能）超越了GRPO和基于特权信息的RL基线。

Conclusion: 结构化的、上下文驱动的探索可以作为暴力rollout扩展和依赖特权信息的可行性导向方法的有效且可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a scalable paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models. However, its effectiveness is fundamentally limited by exploration: the policy can only improve on trajectories it has already sampled. While increasing the number of rollouts alleviates this issue, such brute-force scaling is computationally expensive, and existing approaches that modify the optimization objective provide limited control over what is explored. In this work, we propose NudgeRL, a framework for structured and diversity-driven exploration in RLVR. Our approach introduces Strategy Nudging, which conditions each rollout on lightweight, strategy-level contexts to induce diverse reasoning trajectories without relying on expensive oracle supervision. To effectively learn from such structured exploration, we further propose a unified objective, which decomposes the reward signal into inter- and intra-context components and incorporates a distillation objective to transfer discovered behaviors back to the base policy. Empirically, NudgeRL outperforms standard GRPO with up to 8 times larger rollout budgets, while outperforming oracle-guided RL baseline on average across five challenging math benchmarks. These results demonstrate that structured, context-driven exploration can serve as an efficient and scalable alternative to both brute-force rollout scaling and feasibility-oriented methods based on privileged information. Our code is available at https://github.com/tally0818/NudgeRL.

</details>


### [174] [Can We Trust AI-Inferred User States. A Psychometric Framework for Validating the Reliability of Users States Classification by LLMs in Operational Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15734)
*Izabella Krzeminska,Michal Butkiewicz,Ewa Komkowska*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究发现，大型语言模型在评估用户状态时，其使用的指标在个体得分层面普遍缺乏心理测量学可靠性（213个指标中仅31个达标），并提出了一个可复现的评估框架来验证指标适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑并检验"大语言模型评估用户状态的指标在个体得分层面是稳定且可解释的"这一核心假设。

Method: 采用复制评估程序，对三种双模态大语言模型（GPT-4o audio、Gemini 2.0 Flash、Gemini 2.5 Flash）的广泛指标集进行个体得分信度与聚合信度分析。

Result: 213个指标中仅有31个满足个体层面信度标准；个体得分不稳定但聚合后稳定的指标在后验分析中仍有分析价值；但无法用于实时自适应系统的用户状态推断。

Conclusion: 指标信度不能被视为解释性领域的默认属性；提出可复现评估框架以支持更负责任的自适应系统AI设计，要求对结果解释进行显式信度验证并持续监测信度违例情况。

Abstract: The use of large language models to assess user states in conversational and adaptive systems is based on the assumption that the metrics used for such assessment are stable and interpretable at the level of individual scores. This paper empirically tests this assumption, focusing on the psychometric reliability of artificial intelligence (AI) measures of user states.
  This study employed replication evaluation procedures to assess the repeatability of a broad set of metrics across three different bimodal large language models (GPT-4o audio, Gemini 2.0 Flash, Gemini 2.5 Flash). Analyses include both individual score reliability and aggregated reliability, allowing us to distinguish metrics potentially useful for real-time adaptation from those that retain their value only in aggregated analyses.
  The results demonstrate that metric reliability cannot be considered a default property in interpretive domains. The lack of stability at the level of individual scores precludes the interpretation of such scores as indicators of user state in real-time adaptive systems, even if these metrics demonstrate stability after aggregation. At the same time, the study indicates that individually unstable metrics can retain analytical utility in post-hoc studies, identifying rules governing interactions and their relationships with user experience parameters such as satisfaction, trust, and engagement.
  The main contribution of this work, besides quantifying the severity of the problem (only 31 of 213 metrics met the criteria), is the proposal of a replicable evaluation framework, enabling measurable evaluations of metric applicability. This approach supports more responsible AI design of adaptive systems, in which the interpretation of results requires explicit validation of reliability and monitoring for violations over time.

</details>


### [175] [SaaS-Bench: Can Computer-Use Agents Leverage Real-World SaaS to Solve Professional Workflows?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15777)
*Kean Shi,Zihang Li,Tianyi Ma,Zengji Tu,Jialong Wu,Xinbo Xu,Qingyao Yang,Ruoyu Wu,Weichu Xie,Ming Wu,Jason Zeng,Michael Heinrich,Elvis Zhang,Liang Chen,Kuan Li,Baobao Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SaaS-Bench是一个基于23个真实可部署SaaS系统、包含106个任务的基准测试，用于评估计算机使用智能体在专业工作流程中的能力。实验表明，即使最强的LLM智能体端到端任务完成率也不到4%，暴露了在规划、状态跟踪、跨应用上下文维护和错误恢复方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有web和GUI智能体基准测试通常依赖简化设置、孤立任务或短程交互，难以评估智能体在真实专业工作流程中的能力。SaaS环境承载了大量现代数字化工作，天然包含动态系统状态、跨应用协调、领域特定知识和长程依赖，是评估CUA的理想选择。

Method: 构建了23个可部署的SaaS系统，覆盖六个专业领域，包含106个基于现实工作场景的任务。任务需要长程执行，涵盖文本-only和多模态设置，采用加权验证检查点评估任务完成度与部分进展。

Result: 代表性LLM智能体在SaaS-Bench上表现困难，最强模型端到端任务完成率不足4%。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在专业SaaS工作流程中存在显著局限，包括规划、状态跟踪、跨应用上下文维护和错误恢复等方面的不足，需要进一步研究提升。

Abstract: Computer-Using Agents (CUAs) are rapidly extending large language models (LLMs) beyond text-based reasoning toward action execution in more complex environments, such as web browsers and graphical user interfaces (GUIs). However, existing web and GUI agent benchmarks often rely on simplified settings, isolated tasks, or short-horizon interactions, making it difficult to assess capabilities of agents in realistic professional workflows. Software-as-a-Service (SaaS) environments are a natural choice for CUA evaluation, as they host a large share of modern digital work and naturally involve dynamic system states, cross-application coordination, domain-specific knowledge, and long-horizon dependencies. To this end, we introduce SaaS-Bench, a benchmark built on 23 deployable SaaS systems across six professional domains, containing 106 tasks grounded in realistic work scenarios. These tasks require long-horizon execution, cover both text-only and multimodal settings, and are evaluated with weighted verification checkpoints that measure strict task completion and partial progress. Experiments show that representative LLM-based agents struggle on SaaS-Bench, with even the strongest model completing fewer than 4% of tasks end-to-end, exposing limitations in planning, state tracking, cross-application context maintenance, and error recovery. Code are available at https://github.com/UniPat-AI/SaaS-Bench for reproduction.

</details>


### [176] [Agentic Discovery of Neural Architectures: AIRA-Compose and AIRA-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15871)
*Alberto Pepe,Chien-Yu Lin,Despoina Magka,Bilge Acun,Yannan Nellie Wu,Anton Protopopov,Carole-Jean Wu,Yoram Bachrach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出双框架方法（AIRA-Compose 和 AIRA-Design），让LLM智能体自主设计超越标准Transformer的基础模型架构和注意力机制。实验表明，自主发现的14种架构在1B规模预训练后一致优于Llama 3.2等基线，其中AIRAhybrid-D准确率提升3.8%，AIRAformer-C扩展效率比Llama 3.2快71%，Greedy Opus 4.5在Autoresearch基准上超越已发表最优结果，标志着向递归自我改进迈出重要一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLM智能体能否实现递归自我改进，即自主设计下一代基础模型架构，超越当前人工设计的Transformer标准范式。

Method: 提出双框架方法：(1) AIRA-Compose：使用11个智能体在24小时预算内搜索高层计算原语，评估百万参数候选架构并外推至350M、1B、3B规模，产出Transformer族（AIRAformer）和Transformer-Mamba混合族（AIRAhybrid）共14种架构。(2) AIRA-Design：使用20个智能体编写新型注意力机制和高效训练脚本，针对长程依赖和性能优化。

Result: (1) 下游任务：AIRAformer-D和AIRAhybrid-D准确率分别比Llama 3.2提升2.4%和3.8%。(2) 扩展效率：AIRAformer-C缩放速度比Llama 3.2快54%、比Composer最佳Transformer快71%；AIRAhybrid-C比Nemotron-2快23%、比Composer最佳混合模型快37%。(3) Long Range Arena：智能体设计的架构在文档匹配和文本分类上分别达到人类SOTA的2.3%和2.6%以内。(4) Autoresearch：Greedy Opus 4.5在固定时间预算下达到0.968验证BPB，超越已发表最优。

Conclusion: AI智能体能够自主发现与人工设计基线相当或更优的架构和算法优化，为发现下一代基础模型建立了一种强大范式，标志着向递归自我改进迈出了明确的一步。

Abstract: Toward recursive self-improvement, we investigate LLM agents autonomously designing foundation models beyond standard Transformers. We introduce a dual-framework approach: AIRA-Compose for high-level architecture search, and AIRA-Design for low-level mechanistic implementation. AIRA-Compose uses 11 agents to explore fundamental computational primitives under a 24-hour budget. Agents evaluate million-parameter candidates, extrapolating top designs to 350M, 1B, and 3B scales. This yields 14 architectures across two families: AIRAformers (Transformer-based) and AIRAhybrids (Transformer-Mamba). Pre-trained at 1B scale, these consistently outperform Llama 3.2 and Composer-found baselines. On downstream tasks, AIRAformer-D and AIRAhybrid-D improve accuracy by 2.4% and 3.8% over Llama 3.2. Furthermore, AIRA-Compose finds models with highly efficient scaling frontiers: AIRAformer-C scales 54% and 71% faster than Llama 3.2 and Composer's best Transformer, while AIRAhybrid-C outscales Nemotron-2 by 23% and Composer's best hybrid by 37%. AIRA-Design tasks 20 agents with writing novel attention mechanisms for long-range dependencies and high-performing training scripts. On the Long Range Arena benchmark, agent-designed architectures reach within 2.3% and 2.6% of human state-of-the-art on document matching and text classification. On the Autoresearch benchmark, Greedy Opus 4.5 achieves 0.968 validation bits-per-byte under a fixed time budget, surpassing the published minimum. Together, these frameworks show AI agents can autonomously discover architectures and algorithmic optimizations matching or surpassing hand-designed baselines. This establishes a powerful paradigm for discovering next-generation foundation models, marking a clear step toward recursive self-improvement.

</details>


### [177] [Imperfect World Models are Exploitable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15960)
*Logan Mondal Bhamidipaty,Esmeralda S. Whitammer,David Abel,Mykel J. Kochenderfer,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了强化学习中模型利用（model exploitation）的新定义，建立了奖励黑客（reward hacking）与模型利用之间的形式化桥梁，并揭示了世界模型中安全规划的理论极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对奖励黑客有明确的定义和不可避免性证明，但模型利用缺乏类似的形式化理论。作者希望填补这一空白，并探究两者之间的深层关系。

Method: 首先类比奖励黑客的定义给出模型利用的形式化定义；通过反例证明模型利用的不可避免性不能直接由奖励黑客的证明导出；随后发展统一的通用理论，证明在大策略集上模型利用本质上是不可避免的，并指出奖励黑客是其特例。进一步分析有限策略集上保证无黑客攻击的条件无法推广到模型利用场景，最后引入松弛的模型利用概念并推导其可避免的安全视界。

Result: 1) 建立了模型利用的形式化定义；2) 证明了奖励黑客的不可避免性证明不能直接迁移到模型利用；3) 发展了统一理论，证明大策略集上模型利用不可避免；4) 发现有限策略集上避免黑客攻击的条件不能避免模型利用；5) 提出了松弛的模型利用概念及其安全视界。

Conclusion: 本文在奖励黑客与模型利用之间建立了形式化桥梁，揭示了世界模型中安全规划的理论极限，为理解强化学习中世界模型的风险提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We propose a novel definition of model exploitation in reinforcement learning. Informally, a world model is exploitable if it implies that one policy should be strictly preferred over another while the environment's true transition model implies the reverse. We analogize our definition with a prior characterization of reward hacking but show that the associated proof of inevitability does not transfer to exploitation. To overcome this obstruction, we develop a general theory of reward hacking and model exploitation that proves that exploitation is essentially unavoidable on large policy sets and yields the corresponding claim for hacking as a special case. Unfortunately, we also find that the conditions that guarantee unhackability in finite policy sets have no counterpart that precludes exploitation. Consequently, we introduce a relaxed notion of exploitation and derive a safe horizon within which it can be avoided. Taken together, our results establish a formal bridge between reward hacking and model exploitation and elucidate the limits of safe planning in world models.

</details>


### [178] [PAGER: Bridging the Semantic-Execution Gap in Point-Precise Geometric GUI Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15963)
*Jingxuan Wei,Xi Bai,Shan Liu,Caijun Jia,Zheng Sun,Xinglong Xu,Siyuan Li,Linzhuang Sun,Bihui Yu,Conghui He,Cheng Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文聚焦于精确敏感的GUI几何构造任务，提出PAGE Bench基准（4,906个问题，22.4万个像素级动作）和PAGER智能体，PAGER通过依赖结构规划与像素级执行，结合像素监督微调与精度对齐强化学习，在通用多模态模型任务成功率不足6%的挑战下，实现任务成功率达4.1倍提升，步骤成功率从9%提升至62%+，达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大视觉语言模型在GUI智能体中主要依赖区域容忍范式（同一组件内邻近像素有效），但精确几何构造打破了这一假设——动作必须落在连续画布空间的精确点上，且几何基元存在本体依赖关系，局部坐标误差会引发级联拓扑失效。为此需要定义精确敏感的GUI任务，并建立相应基准和方法。

Method: 提出PAGER智能体，将构造任务分解为依赖结构规划与像素级执行两个阶段；采用像素级监督微调建立可执行动作语法，并通过精度对齐的强化学习（利用状态条件化几何反馈）缓解rollout导致的暴露偏差。

Result: 发现显著的语义-执行鸿沟：通用多模态模型动作类型准确率超88%，但任务成功率低于6%。PAGER将任务成功率提升4.1倍（相对于最强通用基线），步骤成功率从9%（专用GUI智能体）提升至62%以上，达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: 精确敏感的GUI任务与区域容忍任务存在本质差异，PAGER的拓扑感知分解与精度对齐训练机制有效弥合了语义-执行鸿沟，为点级精确GUI控制建立了新标杆。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have significantly advanced GUI agents, enabling executable interaction across web, mobile, and desktop interfaces. Yet these gains largely rely on a forgiving region-tolerant paradigm, where many nearby pixels inside the same component remain valid. Precise geometric construction breaks this assumption: actions must land on points in continuous canvas space rather than tolerant regions. Because geometric primitives carry ontological dependencies, a local coordinate error can induce cascading topological failures that distort downstream objects and invalidate the final construction. We identify this regime as precision-sensitive GUI tasks, requiring point-level accuracy, geometry-aware verification, and robustness to dependency-driven error propagation. To benchmark it, we introduce PAGE Bench, with 4,906 problems and over 224K process-supervised, pixel-level GUI actions. We further propose PAGER, a topology-aware agent that decomposes construction into dependency-structured planning and pixel-level execution. Pixel-grounded supervised tuning establishes executable action grammar, while precision-aligned reinforcement learning mitigates rollout-induced exposure bias through state-conditioned geometric feedback. Experiments reveal a pronounced Semantic-Execution Gap: general multimodal models can exceed 88% action type accuracy yet remain below 6% task success. PAGER closes this gap, delivering 4.1x higher task success than the strongest evaluated general baseline and raising step success rate from below 9% for GUI-specialized agents to over 62%, establishing a new state of the art for point-precise GUI control.

</details>


### [179] [Deterministic Event-Graph Substrates as World Models for Counterfactual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15967)
*Fabio Rovai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出事件图基底（event-graph substrates）——一种基于只追加RDF三元组日志和结构化干预分叉机制的世界模型，支持细粒度可检查性、精确反事实推理和跨域迁移。在CLEVRER数据集（n=75,618）上，基于1,400行DSL解释器的基底超过NS-DR符号基线所有四个问题类别（分别高9.89、20.26、17.65、0.80个百分点），在描述性和解释性问题上超过参数化ALOE基线，在预测性和反事实问题上略逊。同时引入twin-EventLog基准（500个Smallville反事实规范），基底联合准确率比全上下文Llama-3.1-8B高18.80个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型要么依赖学习组件导致可解释性和精确反事实推理受限，要么在跨域迁移上表现不佳。作者希望构建一种可完全检查、支持精确反事实、且无需学习组件即可跨域迁移的世界模型表示形式。

Method: 定义事件图基底形式化类，采用只追加的RDF三元组日志表示智能体状态，通过结构化干预词汇表下的日志分叉（fork）来回答反事实查询。证明了解释性查询与反事实查询之间的对偶性，将两者归约为相同的因果祖先遍历。基于1,400行CLEVRER-DSL解释器在领域无关的基底运行时上实现全规模评估。

Result: 在CLEVRER（n=75,618）上，基底超过NS-DR符号基线全部四个问题类别（描述性+9.89、解释性+20.26、预测性+17.65、反事实+0.80个百分点）；在描述性和解释性上超过ALOE参数化基线，在预测性和反事实上落后。在twin-EventLog基准（500个规范）上，基底联合准确率比全上下文Llama-3.1-8B高18.80个百分点。

Conclusion: 事件图基底作为一种符号化、可检查、支持精确反事实的世界模型表示，在多个任务上表现优于或可比于现有符号基线和部分参数化方法，展示了无需学习组件即可实现强泛化和跨域迁移的潜力。

Abstract: We study event-graph substrates: a class of world models that represent agent state as an append-only log of typed RDF triples and answer counterfactual queries by forking the log under a structured intervention vocabulary. Substrates are inspectable at the triple level, support exact counterfactuals, and transfer across domains without learned components. We formalize the class, prove a duality between explanatory and counterfactual queries that reduces both to the same causal-ancestor traversal, and evaluate a 1,400-line CLEVRER-DSL interpreter atop a domain-agnostic substrate runtime at full CLEVRER validation scale (n=75,618). The substrate exceeds the NS-DR symbolic oracle on all four per-question categories (by 9.89, 20.26, 17.65, and 0.80 percentage points), and exceeds the parametric ALOE baseline on descriptive and explanatory while lagging on predictive and counterfactual. We also introduce twin-EventLog, a 500-specification Park-canonical Smallville counterfactual benchmark on which the substrate exceeds Llama-3.1-8B with full context by 18.80 points joint accuracy.

</details>


### [180] [Learning Bilevel Policies over Symbolic World Models for Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15975)
*Dillon Z. Chen,Till Hofmann,Toryn Q. Klassen,Sheila A. McIlraith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BISON提出双层策略架构，结合底层模仿学习与高层符号抽象，实现长时域具身智能体规划，在MetaWorld基准上优于VLA和端到端方法，在训练/推理时更高效，且高层策略可在一分钟内解决含10,000个对象的规划问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习在细粒度操作任务上表现优异，但难以独立生成长时域规划；高层符号抽象擅长长时域规划但缺乏底层控制能力。现有方法(VLA/端到端)难以兼顾两者优势，因此需要一种将底层模仿学习与高层符号规划结合的框架来解决长时域具身规划问题。

Method: 提出BISON系统，基于双层策略(π^hl, π^ll)：π^ll为从底层演示中学得的神经策略，负责操控与连续控制；π^hl为从底层演示的符号抽象结合归纳泛化构建的高层符号策略。两层策略协同工作，HL负责长时域规划，LL负责具体执行。

Result: 在扩展的MetaWorld基准上，BISON在长时域和更多物体数量的场景下均优于VLA和端到端方法；在训练和推理阶段更省时、更节省内存；忽略底层执行时，高层策略可在一分钟内解决具有10,000个相关物体的高层规划问题。

Conclusion: 将底层模仿学习与高层符号抽象结合的双层策略范式是解决长时域具身规划问题的有效途径，兼具底层操控精度与高层规划可扩展性，在效率和泛化能力上显著超越纯端到端方法。

Abstract: We tackle the challenge of building embodied AI agents that can reliably solve long-horizon planning problems. Imitation learning from demonstrations has shown itself to be effective in training robots to solve a diversity of complex tasks requiring fine motor control and manipulation over low-level (LL), continuous environments. Yet, it remains a difficult endeavour to generate long-horizon plans from imitation learning alone. In contrast, high-level (HL), symbolic abstractions facilitate efficient and interpretable long-horizon planning. We propose to combine the strengths of LL imitation learning for manipulation and control, and HL symbolic abstractions for long-horizon planning. We realise this idea via \emph{bilevel policies} of the form $(π^{\mathrm{hl}}, π^{\mathrm{ll}})$, consisting of a neural policy $π^{\mathrm{ll}}$ learned from LL demonstrations, and an HL symbolic policy $π^{\mathrm{hl}}$ that is constructed from symbolic abstractions of the LL demonstrations combined with inductive generalisation. We implement these ideas in the BISON system. Experiments on extended MetaWorld benchmarks demonstrate that BISON generalises to long horizons and problems with greater numbers of objects than those solved by VLA and end-to-end methods, and is more time and memory efficient in training and inference. Notably, when ignoring LL execution, BISON's HL policies can solve HL problems with 10,000 relevant objects in under a minute. Project page: https://dillonzchen.github.io/bison

</details>


### [181] [Petri Net Induced Heuristic Search for Resource Constrained Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15983)
*Ido Lublin,Dor Atzmon,Izack Cohen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将资源受限项目调度问题（RCPSP）建模为带资源的时间迁移Petri网可达性图上的最优搜索，使用相对延迟令牌使调度决策对应状态空间中的变迁触发，通过结合关键路径和资源下界的A*启发式求解，实验表明该方法在成功率和求解时间上均优于强MIP基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RCPSP求解方法（如MIP）在问题规模增大时性能显著下降，需要更高效的求解框架。

Method: 将RCPSP建模为带资源的时序变迁Petri网（TTPNR）的可达性图搜索问题；引入相对延迟令牌以映射调度决策为状态空间中的变迁触发；采用A*算法，结合关键路径法与资源下界构造一致启发式函数。

Result: 在PSPLIB基准测试上，该方法在成功率和求解时间上均优于SCIP和CBC等强MIP求解器；A*的性能瓶颈为资源紧度，MIP的瓶颈为问题规模，资源强度调节了各自优势区间。

Conclusion: 基于Petri网可达性图的A*搜索是求解RCPSP的有效框架，与MIP形成互补优势，在不同资源强度条件下各有适用场景。

Abstract: We formulate the Resource-Constrained Project Scheduling Problem (RCPSP) as optimal search over the reachability graph of a Timed Transition Petri Net with Resources, using relative-delay tokens so that scheduling decisions correspond to transition firings in the induced state space. We solve the resulting problem with $A^*$ guided by a heuristic that combines Critical Path and resource-based lower bounds, and prove that it is consistent under our token-based time semantics. Experiments on the PSPLIB benchmarks show that the approach outperforms strong exact Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MIP) baselines (SCIP, CBC) in both success rate and solve time. Per-instance analysis shows that heuristic search and MIP degrade along independent axes, resource tightness for $A^*$ and formulation size for MIP, with resource strength mediating which solver benefits from scale.

</details>


### [182] [Reasoners or Translators? Contamination-aware Evaluation and Neuro-Symbolic Robustness in Tax Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16052)
*Parisa Kordjamshidi,Samer Aslan,Madhavan Seshadri,Leslie Barrett,Enrico Santus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在税法推理中的表现，发现数据污染会虚增性能。通过系统性评估，表明神经符号混合框架比纯LLM更可靠、鲁棒，尤其在泛化到未见文档和组合性法律推理方面表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在法律推理中的表现是否真正源自推理能力，还是数据污染的人为假象，以评估其可靠性。

Method: 实现污染检测协议；构建包含案例和规则变体的新型测试集；对比纯LLM与将法规文本翻译为形式表示并委托符号求解器推理的混合系统。

Result: 数据污染会虚增LLM性能；法律推理本质上是组合性的；神经符号框架在可靠性和泛化能力上优于纯LLM。

Conclusion: 神经符号框架为法律AI提供了更可靠、鲁棒的基础，能够更好地泛化到未见情境。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have significantly enhanced automated legal reasoning. Yet, it remains unclear whether their performance reflects genuine legal reasoning ability or artifacts of data contamination. We present a comprehensive empirical study of tax law reasoning approaches and implement a contamination detection protocol to rigorously assess LLM reliability. We show that performance can be inflated by contamination. Building on this analysis, we conduct a systematic evaluation, comparing monolithic LLMs with hybrid systems that translate statutory text into formal representations and delegate inference to symbolic solvers. We build a novel test suite designed to probe generalization to unseen documents via case and rule variations. Our findings indicate that legal reasoning is inherently compositional and that neuro-symbolic frameworks offer a more reliable and robust foundation for legal AI, as well as improved generalization to unobserved situations.

</details>


### [183] [Sign-Separated Finite-Time Error Analysis of Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16103)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对恒定步长Q-learning进行了符号分离的有限时间误差分析，通过切换系统表示将误差分解为正负两部分，揭示了Q-learning误差动态中的最大值诱导不对称性，并提供了确定性和随机递归的有限时间界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Q-learning收敛分析多为渐近结果或对称误差界，未能捕捉误差动态中正负分量的非对称行为，特别是与过估计现象相关的内在不对称性。

Method: 将Q-learning误差动态表示为切换系统，将误差分解为分量负部和正部；负部由固定最优策略下的较低比较LTI系统主导，正部由线性切换系统控制；分别推导确定性和随机恒定步长递归的有限时间界。

Result: 负侧LTI证书的收敛速率不慢于正侧切换证书，且可能产生更快的指数包络；识别出Q-learning误差动态中的最大值诱导不对称性，正动作误差可被Bellman最大值选择并传播，而负误差受最优策略较低比较约束。

Conclusion: 该分析为Q-learning的过估计现象提供了理论解释，建立了误差正负分量非对称动力学与算法行为之间的形式化联系。

Abstract: This paper develops a sign-separated finite-time error analysis for constant step-size Q-learning. Starting from the switching-system representation, the error is decomposed into its componentwise negative and positive parts. The negative part is dominated by a lower comparison linear time-invariant (LTI) system associated with a fixed optimal policy, whereas the positive part is controlled by a linear switching system. The resulting bounds show that the negative-side LTI certificate is no slower than the positive-side switching certificate and may produce a faster exponential envelope. The analysis identifies a max-induced asymmetry in Q-learning error dynamics. This asymmetry is connected to overestimation: positive action-wise errors can be selected and propagated by the Bellman maximum, whereas negative errors admit an optimal-policy lower comparison. Finite-time bounds are provided for both deterministic and stochastic constant-step-size recursions.

</details>


### [184] [ShopGym: An Integrated Framework for Realistic Simulation and Scalable Benchmarking of E-Commerce Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16116)
*Chinmay Savadikar,Mingyu Zhao,Yuanzheng Zhu,Han Li,Shuang Xie,Alberto Castelo,Tianfu Wu,Lingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ShopGym是一个用于电商网页智能体评估的集成框架，通过ShopArena将真实网店转换为可控沙箱环境，并结合ShopGuru合成跨7类技能的基准任务，实现了仿真性、多样性、可控性和可复现性的统一。实验表明合成商店保留了真实商店的结构属性，且智能体在两者上的表现正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电商网页智能体评估方法存在权衡困境：真实商店环境具有非平稳、难以检查、不可复现的问题，而手工构建的沙箱基准则覆盖范围狭窄。领域缺乏一种可扩展的方法来构建同时具备真实性、多样性、可控性、可检查性和可复现性的评估环境。

Method: ShopGym包含两个核心组件：1) ShopArena——通过匿名化商店规格和分阶段验证的生成流程，将真实种子商店转换为自包含的沙箱商店；2) ShopGuru——基于商店的目录、导航结构、策略和交互能力，合成覆盖7个技能类别的基准任务。两者共同产生自包含、可重置、可检查且稳定的评估工件。

Result: 在包含224个生成任务的6个沙箱商店（3个合成数据、3个真实数据）上，通过基于图的结构分析和基于智能体的行为评估验证：合成商店保留了真实商店的关键结构属性，且智能体在合成商店上的性能与在真实商店上的表现呈正相关。

Conclusion: ShopGym填补了电商网页智能体评估的方法论空白，提供了一种可扩展的框架来构建兼具真实性和可控性的评估环境，为未来电商智能体研究奠定了标准化基准基础。

Abstract: Developing and evaluating e-commerce web agents requires environments that preserve meaningful task structure while enabling controllable, reproducible, and scalable scientific comparison. Existing methodologies force a tradeoff: live storefronts provide realism but are non-stationary, difficult to inspect, and irreproducible, while hand-built sandbox benchmarks provide control but cover only a narrow range of layouts, catalogs, policies, and interaction patterns. We argue that the core bottleneck is methodological: the field lacks a scalable way to construct evaluation settings that are simultaneously realistic, diverse, controllable, inspectable, and reproducible. We introduce ShopGym, an integrated framework for realistic simulation and scalable benchmarking of e-commerce web agents. ShopGym is a framework for constructing e-commerce simulation environments and grounded benchmark tasks. Its simulation layer, ShopArena, converts live seed storefronts into self-contained sandbox shops through anonymized shop specifications and a staged, validated generation process. On top of these simulated storefronts, ShopGuru synthesizes benchmark tasks across seven skill categories, grounding each task in the shop's catalog, navigation structure, policies, and interaction affordances. Together, ShopArena and ShopGuru produce self-contained, resettable, inspectable, and stable evaluation artifacts that preserve structural properties and agent-evaluation signals relevant to shopping tasks. We validate the framework through graph-based structural analysis and agent-based behavioral evaluation with 224 generated tasks across six sandbox shops: three constructed with synthetic data and three with real data. Our results show that the synthetic shops preserve key structural properties of live storefronts, with agent performance on synthetic shops positively correlated with performance on live storefronts.

</details>


### [185] [Property-Guided LLM Program Synthesis for Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16142)
*Augusto B. Corrêa,André G. Pereira,Jendrik Seipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出属性引导的LLM程序合成方法，通过形式化属性验证和具体反例反馈替代传统评分机制，在PDDL规划领域合成直接启发式函数。该方法相比之前方法平均每个领域生成程序数量减少7倍，无需搜索即可解决更多任务，且评估计算量降低数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM程序合成依赖简单数值评分（如解决方案价值或通过测试数）来指示程序质量，但评分无法解释程序为何失败，导致系统需要生成和评估大量候选程序，增加了LLM推理和评估成本。

Method: 属性引导的LLM程序合成：检查候选程序是否满足形式化定义的属性，当属性被违反时提前停止评估，并向LLM提供具体反例展示程序失败的具体原因。采用反例引导的修复循环，生成一个候选程序后在训练集上检查属性，返回第一个违反属性的案例。

Result: 在十个PDDL规划领域上评估，合成启发式在几乎所有测试任务上都有效。相比最佳先前方法，平均每个领域生成程序数量减少7倍，无需搜索即可解决更多任务，评估候选程序所需计算量降低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 只要问题存在可验证的属性，属性引导的LLM程序合成就能降低计算成本并提高程序质量。

Abstract: LLMs have shown impressive success in program synthesis, discovering programs that surpass prior solutions. However, these approaches rely on simple numeric scores to signal program quality, such as the value of the solution or the number of passed tests. Because a score offers no guidance on why a program failed, the system must generate and evaluate many candidates hoping some succeed, increasing LLM inference and evaluation costs. We study a different approach: property-guided LLM program synthesis. Instead of scoring programs after evaluation, we check whether a candidate satisfies a formally defined property. When the property is violated, we stop the evaluation early and provide the LLM with a concrete counterexample showing exactly how the program failed. This feedback drastically reduces both the number of program generations and the evaluation cost, and can guide the LLM to generate stronger programs. We evaluate this approach on PDDL planning domains, asking the LLM to synthesize direct heuristic functions: every state reachable by strictly improving transitions has a strictly improving successor. A heuristic with this property leads hill-climbing algorithm directly to a goal state. A counterexample-guided repair loop generates one candidate program, checks the property over a training set, and returns the first case that violates the property. We evaluate our approach on ten planning domains with an out-of-distribution test set. The synthesized heuristics are effectively direct on virtually all test tasks, and compared to the best prior generation method our approach generates seven times fewer programs per domain on average, solves more tasks without using search, and requires several orders of magnitude less computation to evaluate candidates. Whenever a problem admits a verifiable property, property-guided LLM synthesis can reduce cost and improve program quality.

</details>


### [186] [Look Before You Leap: Autonomous Exploration for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16143)
*Ziang Ye,Wentao Shi,Yuxin Liu,Yu Wang,Zhengzhou Cai,Yaorui Shi,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出探索检查点覆盖率（ECC）指标来量化智能体的自主探索能力，并设计了交织任务执行与探索的训练策略，最终提出"先探索后行动"（Explore-then-Act）范式，证明系统化探索对构建通用、可部署于真实世界的智能体至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型智能体在面对陌生环境时常因"过早利用"（premature exploitation）而失败，即在对环境信息收集不足时便依赖先验知识采取行动。自主探索能力虽关键但未被充分研究，亟需形式化定义与量化评估方法。

Method: 1) 提出探索检查点覆盖率（Exploration Checkpoint Coverage, ECC）作为可验证的量化指标，用于衡量智能体探索关键状态、对象和功能的广度。2) 设计交错训练策略，将任务执行轨迹与探索轨迹交织进行，每种轨迹由对应的可验证奖励函数优化。3) 提出Explore-then-Act范式，解耦信息收集与任务执行：智能体先用交互预算获取环境知识，再将其用于任务求解。
